'use server';

import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { getActiveDruzynaId } from '@/lib/druzyny';
import { enforceRateLimit } from '@/lib/rate-limit';
import { z } from 'zod';

const notatkaSchema = z.object({
  notatki: z
    .string()
    .trim()
    .max(2000, 'Notatka moĹĽe mieÄ‡ maksymalnie 2000 znakĂłw')
    .transform((v) => (v === '' ? null : v)),
});

export type FormState = {
  error?: string;
  success?: boolean;
} | null;

export async function zapiszWynikLubNotatke(
  treningId: number,
  formData: FormData
): Promise<FormState> {
  if (!Number.isInteger(treningId) || treningId <= 0) {
    return { error: 'NieprawidĹ‚owe ID treningu.' };
  }

  const supabase = await createClient();

  const {
    data: { user },
  } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

  if (!user) {
    return { error: 'Musisz byÄ‡ zalogowany.' };
  }

  try {
    await enforceRateLimit(
      `user:${user.id}`,
      'zapiszWynikLubNotatke',
      100,
      3600
    );
  } catch (err) {
    return {
      error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Zbyt wiele operacji.',
    };
  }

  const druzynaId = await getActiveDruzynaId();
  if (!druzynaId) {
    return { error: 'Brak aktywnej druĹĽyny.' };
  }

  const raw = {
    notatki: formData.get('notatki') ?? '',
  };

  const parsed = notatkaSchema.safeParse(raw);

  if (!parsed.success) {
    return { error: parsed.error.issues[0].message };
  }

  // FIX: count przekazywany do update(), nie do select()
  const { error, count } = await supabase
    .from('treningi')
    .update({ notatki: parsed.data.notatki }, { count: 'exact' })
    .eq('id', treningId)
    .eq('druzyna_id', druzynaId)
    .select('id');

  if (error) {
    console.error('BĹ‚Ä…d zapisu notatki:', error);
    return { error: 'Nie udaĹ‚o siÄ™ zapisaÄ‡ notatki. SprĂłbuj ponownie.' };
  }

  if (!count || count === 0) {
    return { error: 'Nie znaleziono treningu w aktywnej druĹĽynie.' };
  }

  return { success: true };
}

type TreningZObecnoscia = {
  id: number;
  data: string;
  godzina: string;
  typ: string;
  przeciwnik: string | null;
  miejsce: string | null;
  notatki: string | null;
  cykl_id: string | null;
  obecnosc: { trening_id: number; status: string }[] | null;
};

type StatsMap = Map<number, { obecni: number; nieobecni: number }>;

function formatDzienTyg(date: Date) {
  return date.toLocaleDateString('pl-PL', { weekday: 'short' }).replace('.', '');
}

const typKolor = (typ: string) => {
  if (typ === 'mecz') return 'bg-red-500';
  if (typ === 'sparing') return 'bg-orange-500';
  if (typ === 'turniej') return 'bg-purple-500';
  return 'bg-blue-500';
};
const typBorder = (typ: string) => {
  if (typ === 'mecz') return 'border-l-red-500';
  if (typ === 'sparing') return 'border-l-orange-500';
  if (typ === 'turniej') return 'border-l-purple-500';
  return 'border-l-blue-500';
};

  nextWeek: string;

                </Link>
              </>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

  return (
    <main className="relative min-h-screen w-full overflow-hidden bg-slate-950">

                <div>
                  <div className="w-32 h-6 rounded-lg bg-slate-800/70 animate-pulse mb-2" />
                  <div className="w-24 h-3 rounded bg-slate-800/50 animate-pulse" />
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-slate-800/70 animate-pulse" />

            {/* Legenda */}
            <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-4 mt-4 pt-3 border-t border-white/10">
              {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
                <div key={i} className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <div className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-slate-800/70 animate-pulse" />
                  <div className="w-12 h-3 rounded bg-slate-800/50 animate-pulse" />
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Przyciski */}
          <div className="flex gap-2 flex-wrap mb-5">
            <div className="flex-1 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-slate-800/60 animate-pulse" />
            <div className="w-24 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-slate-800/60 animate-pulse" />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

=== PLIK: C:\Users\AdamPilny\Desktop\talent\Aplikacja-trenera-pi-karskiego-main\app\(app)\dashboard\page.tsx ===
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { getActiveDruzynaId, getActiveDruzyna } from '@/lib/druzyny';
import { getDzisISO, getMonday, addDays, toISO } from '@/lib/dates';
import { DashboardClient } from './dashboardClient';
import { TopBar } from '@/components/TopBar';

type TreningZObecnoscia = {
  id: number;
  data: string;
  godzina: string;
  typ: string;
  przeciwnik: string | null;
  miejsce: string | null;
  notatki: string | null;
  cykl_id: string | null;
  obecnosc: { trening_id: number; status: string }[] | null;
};

export default async function Dashboard({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ w?: string }>;
}) {
  const params = await searchParams;
  const teraz = new Date();
  const dzisStr = getDzisISO();

  let poniedzialek = getMonday(teraz);
  if (params.w) {
    const parsed = new Date(params.w + 'T00:00:00');
    if (!isNaN(parsed.getTime())) {
      poniedzialek = getMonday(parsed);
    }
  }

  const niedziela = addDays(poniedzialek, 6);

  const supabase = await createClient();
  const druzynaId = await getActiveDruzynaId();
  if (!druzynaId) redirect('/druzyny/nowa');

  const activeDruzyna = await getActiveDruzyna();

  const odD = new Date(teraz.getFullYear(), teraz.getMonth() - 1, 1);
  const doD = new Date(teraz.getFullYear(), teraz.getMonth() + 2, 0);

  const [treningiRes] = await Promise.all([
    supabase
      .from('treningi')
      .select(`
        id, data, godzina, typ, przeciwnik, miejsce, notatki, cykl_id,
        obecnosc (trening_id, status)
      `)
      .eq('druzyna_id', druzynaId)
      .gte('data', toISO(odD))
      .lte('data', toISO(doD))
      .order('data', { ascending: true })
      .order('godzina', { ascending: true }),
  ]);

  if (treningiRes.error) {
    console.error('BĹ‚Ä…d pobierania treningĂłw:', treningiRes.error);
  }

  const lista = (treningiRes.data ?? []) as unknown as TreningZObecnoscia[];

  const wszystkieObecnosci = lista.flatMap((t) => t.obecnosc ?? []);

  const stats = new Map<number, { obecni: number; nieobecni: number }>();
  lista.forEach((t) => {
    let obecni = 0;
    let nieobecni = 0;
    for (const o of t.obecnosc ?? []) {
      if (o.status === 'obecny' || o.status === 'spozniony') obecni++;
      else nieobecni++;
    }
    stats.set(t.id, { obecni, nieobecni });
  });

  const dni = Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, i) => addDays(poniedzialek, i));

  const eventMap: Record<string, TreningZObecnoscia[]> = {};
  lista.forEach((t) => {
    if (!eventMap[t.data]) eventMap[t.data] = [];
    eventMap[t.data].push(t);
  });

  const jestWTygodniu =
    dzisStr >= toISO(poniedzialek) && dzisStr <= toISO(niedziela);

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-screen-2xl mx-auto">
      <DashboardClient
        initialDni={dni}
        poniedzialekISO={toISO(poniedzialek)}
        niedzielaISO={toISO(niedziela)}
        prevWeek={toISO(addDays(poniedzialek, -7))}
        nextWeek={toISO(addDays(poniedzialek, 7))}
        jestWTygodniu={jestWTygodniu}
        eventMap={eventMap}
        stats={stats}
        wszystkieObecnosci={wszystkieObecnosci}
        topBar={<TopBar />}
        logoUrl={activeDruzyna?.logo_url ?? null}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

=== PLIK: C:\Users\AdamPilny\Desktop\talent\Aplikacja-trenera-pi-karskiego-main\app\(app)\druzyny\nowa\actions.ts ===
'use server';

import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';
import { revalidatePath, revalidateTag } from 'next/cache';
import {
  ACTIVE_DRUZYNA_COOKIE,
  DRUZYNY_CACHE_TAG,
  ZAWODNICY_CACHE_TAG,
} from '@/lib/druzyny';
import { enforceRateLimit } from '@/lib/rate-limit';
import { dodajDruzynaSchema, parseFormData } from '@/lib/schemas';

export async function dodajDruzyna(formData: FormData) {
  const supabase = await createClient();

  const {
    data: { user },
  } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

  if (!user) {
    throw new Error('Musisz byc zalogowany.');
  }

  await enforceRateLimit(`user:${user.id}`, 'dodajDruzyna', 10, 3600);

  const parsed = parseFormData(dodajDruzynaSchema, formData);
  const { nazwa, rocznik, kolor } = parsed;

  const { data: nowa, error } = await supabase
    .from('druzyny')
    .insert({
      user_id: user.id,
      nazwa,
      rocznik,
      kolor,
    })
    .select('id')
    .single();

  if (error || !nowa) {
    throw new Error(error?.message ?? 'Nie udalo sie utworzyc druzyny.');
  }

  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  cookieStore.set(ACTIVE_DRUZYNA_COOKIE, nowa.id, {
    httpOnly: true,
    sameSite: 'lax',
    path: '/',
    maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24 * 365,
    secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production',
  });

  revalidateTag(DRUZYNY_CACHE_TAG, 'default');
  revalidateTag(ZAWODNICY_CACHE_TAG, 'default');
  revalidatePath('/', 'layout');
  redirect('/druzyny?toast=druzyna_dodana');
}

=== PLIK: C:\Users\AdamPilny\Desktop\talent\Aplikacja-trenera-pi-karskiego-main\app\(app)\druzyny\[id]\edytuj\actions.ts ===
'use server';

import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';
import { revalidatePath, revalidateTag } from 'next/cache';
import {
  ACTIVE_DRUZYNA_COOKIE,
  DRUZYNY_CACHE_TAG,
  ZAWODNICY_CACHE_TAG,
} from '@/lib/druzyny';
import { enforceRateLimit } from '@/lib/rate-limit';
import { dodajDruzynaSchema, parseFormData } from '@/lib/schemas';
import { z } from 'zod';

// P1.2: Walidacja formatu UUID
const uuidSchema = z.string().uuid();

export async function aktualizujDruzyna(id: string, formData: FormData) {
  if (!uuidSchema.safeParse(id).success) {
    throw new Error('NieprawidĹ‚owe ID druĹĽyny.');
  }

  const supabase = await createClient();
  const {
    data: { user },
  } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

  if (!user) {
    throw new Error('Musisz byc zalogowany.');
  }

  await enforceRateLimit(`user:${user.id}`, 'aktualizujDruzyna', 20, 3600);

  const parsed = parseFormData(dodajDruzynaSchema, formData);
  const { nazwa, rocznik, kolor } = parsed;

  // P1.2: Dodanie .eq('user_id', user.id) dla Defense in Depth
  const { error } = await supabase
    .from('druzyny')
    .update({ nazwa, rocznik, kolor })
    .eq('id', id)
    .eq('user_id', user.id);

  if (error) {
    throw new Error(error.message);
  }

  revalidateTag(DRUZYNY_CACHE_TAG, 'default');
  revalidatePath('/', 'layout');
  revalidatePath('/druzyny');
  redirect('/druzyny?toast=druzyna_zapisana');
}

export async function usunDruzyna(formData: FormData) {
  const id = formData.get('id') as string;

  if (!id || !uuidSchema.safeParse(id).success) {
    throw new Error('NieprawidĹ‚owe ID druĹĽyny.');
  }

  const supabase = await createClient();
  const {
    data: { user },
  } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

  if (!user) {
    throw new Error('Musisz byc zalogowany.');
  }

  await enforceRateLimit(`user:${user.id}`, 'usunDruzyna', 5, 3600);

  // P1.2: Filtr user_id przy sprawdzaniu istnienia
  const { data: druzyna, error: errFetch } = await supabase
    .from('druzyny')
    .select('id')
    .eq('id', id)
    .eq('user_id', user.id)
    .single();

  if (errFetch || !druzyna) {
    throw new Error('Nie znaleziono druzyny.');
  }

  // P1.2: Filtr user_id przy usuwaniu
  const { error } = await supabase
    .from('druzyny')
    .delete()
    .eq('id', id)
    .eq('user_id', user.id);

  if (error) {
    throw new Error(error.message);
  }

  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  const activeId = cookieStore.get(ACTIVE_DRUZYNA_COOKIE)?.value;

  if (activeId === id) {
    const { data: pozostale } = await supabase
      .from('druzyny')
      .select('id')
      .eq('user_id', user.id) // P1.2: zawÄ™ĹĽenie do druĹĽyn uĹĽytkownika
      .order('created_at', { ascending: true })
      .limit(1);

    if (pozostale && pozostale.length > 0) {
      cookieStore.set(ACTIVE_DRUZYNA_COOKIE, pozostale[0].id, {
        httpOnly: true,
        sameSite: 'lax',
        path: '/',
        maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24 * 365,
        secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production',
      });
    } else {
      cookieStore.delete(ACTIVE_DRUZYNA_COOKIE);
    }
  }

  revalidateTag(DRUZYNY_CACHE_TAG, 'default');
  revalidateTag(ZAWODNICY_CACHE_TAG, 'default');
  revalidatePath('/', 'layout');
  revalidatePath('/druzyny');
  redirect('/druzyny?toast=druzyna_usunieta');
}

=== PLIK: C:\Users\AdamPilny\Desktop\talent\Aplikacja-trenera-pi-karskiego-main\app\(app)\kadra\page.tsx ===
import Link from 'next/link';
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { getActiveDruzynaId, getActiveDruzyna } from '@/lib/druzyny';
import { getDzisISO } from '@/lib/dates';
import { TopBar } from '@/components/TopBar';
import { PageHeaderLogo } from '@/components/PageHeaderLogo';
import { Users, Plus, Search, ArrowUpDown, Upload } from 'lucide-react';

type Zawodnik = {
  id: number;
  imie: string;
  nazwisko: string;
  rocznik: number | null;
  pozycja: string | null;
  numer: number | null;
  kontakt_rodzica: string | null;
  notatki: string | null;
};

type SortKey = 'alpha' | 'rocznik' | 'frekwencja' | 'numer';

const SORT_LABELS: Record<SortKey, string> = {
  alpha: 'Alfabetycznie',
  rocznik: 'Rocznik',
  frekwencja: 'Frekwencja',
  numer: 'Numer',
};

function kolorKropki(status: string): string {
  if (status === 'obecny' || status === 'spozniony') return 'bg-green-400';
  if (status === 'nieobecny' || status === 'usprawiedliwiony') return 'bg-red-400';
  return 'bg-white/15';
}

function labelStatusu(status: string): string {
  if (status === 'obecny') return 'Obecny';
  if (status === 'spozniony') return 'SpĂłĹşniony';
  if (status === 'nieobecny') return 'Nieobecny';
  if (status === 'usprawiedliwiony') return 'Usprawiedliwiony';
  return 'Brak wpisu';
}

export default async function Kadra({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ q?: string; sort?: string }>;
}) {
  const params = await searchParams;
  const q = params.q?.trim() || '';
  const sortParam = params.sort as SortKey | undefined;
  const sort: SortKey = ['alpha', 'rocznik', 'frekwencja', 'numer'].includes(sortParam ?? '')
    ? (sortParam as SortKey)
    : 'alpha';

  const supabase = await createClient();
  const druzynaId = await getActiveDruzynaId();
  if (!druzynaId) redirect('/druzyny/nowa');

  const activeDruzyna = await getActiveDruzyna();

  const dzisISO = getDzisISO();

  const { data: ostatnieWydarzenia } = await supabase
    .from('treningi')
    .select('id, data, godzina, typ')
    .eq('druzyna_id', druzynaId)
    .lte('data', dzisISO)
    .order('data', { ascending: false })
    .order('godzina', { ascending: false })
    .limit(5);

  const last5 = ostatnieWydarzenia ?? [];
  const last5Ids = last5.map((t) => t.id);

  let zawQuery = supabase
    .from('zawodnicy')
    .select('id, imie, nazwisko, rocznik, pozycja, numer, kontakt_rodzica, notatki')
    .eq('druzyna_id', druzynaId);

  if (q) {
    const safe = q.replace(/[,()%]/g, '\\$&');
    zawQuery = zawQuery.or(`imie.ilike.%${safe}%,nazwisko.ilike.%${safe}%`);
  }

  const [zawodnicyRes, obecnosciRes, frekwencjaRes] = await Promise.all([
    zawQuery,
    last5Ids.length > 0
      ? supabase
          .from('obecnosc')
          .select('zawodnik_id, trening_id, status')
          .in('trening_id', last5Ids)
      : Promise.resolve({
          data: [] as { zawodnik_id: number; trening_id: number; status: string }[],
          error: null,
        }),
    supabase.rpc('get_frekwencja_zawodnikow', { p_druzyna_id: druzynaId }),
  ]);

  const error = zawodnicyRes.error;
  const zawodnicy = (zawodnicyRes.data ?? []) as Zawodnik[];

  const statusMap = new Map<number, Map<number, string>>();
  for (const o of obecnosciRes.data ?? []) {
    if (!statusMap.has(o.zawodnik_id)) statusMap.set(o.zawodnik_id, new Map());
    statusMap.get(o.zawodnik_id)!.set(o.trening_id, o.status);
  }

  const dotsMap = new Map<number, string[]>();
  zawodnicy.forEach((z) => {
    const m = statusMap.get(z.id);
    dotsMap.set(
      z.id,
      last5.map((t) => m?.get(t.id) ?? 'brak')
    );
  });

  const frekwencjaMap = new Map<number, { obecni: number; total: number }>();
  for (const row of (frekwencjaRes.data ?? []) as {
    zawodnik_id: number;
    obecni: number;
    total: number;
  }[]) {
    frekwencjaMap.set(row.zawodnik_id, {
      obecni: Number(row.obecni),
      total: Number(row.total),
    });
  }

  const inicjaly = (imie: string, nazwisko: string) => {
    return `${imie.charAt(0)}${nazwisko.charAt(0)}`.toUpperCase();
  };

  const posortowani = [...zawodnicy].sort((a, b) => {
    if (sort === 'alpha') {
      const cmp = a.nazwisko.localeCompare(b.nazwisko, 'pl');
      if (cmp !== 0) return cmp;
      return a.imie.localeCompare(b.imie, 'pl');
    }
    if (sort === 'rocznik') {
      const ra = a.rocznik ?? 0;
      const rb = b.rocznik ?? 0;
      if (rb !== ra) return rb - ra;
      return a.nazwisko.localeCompare(b.nazwisko, 'pl');
    }
    if (sort === 'frekwencja') {
      const sa = frekwencjaMap.get(a.id) ?? { obecni: 0, total: 0 };
      const sb = frekwencjaMap.get(b.id) ?? { obecni: 0, total: 0 };
      const pa = sa.total > 0 ? sa.obecni / sa.total : 0;
      const pb = sb.total > 0 ? sb.obecni / sb.total : 0;
      if (pb !== pa) return pb - pa;
      return a.nazwisko.localeCompare(b.nazwisko, 'pl');
    }
    const na = a.numer ?? 9999;
    const nb = b.numer ?? 9999;
    if (na !== nb) return na - nb;
    return a.nazwisko.localeCompare(b.nazwisko, 'pl');
  });

  const liczba = posortowani.length;

  const buildSortUrl = (key: SortKey) => {
    const p = new URLSearchParams();
    if (q) p.set('q', q);
    if (key !== 'alpha') p.set('sort', key);
    const qs = p.toString();
    return qs ? `/kadra?${qs}` : '/kadra';
  };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-3xl md:max-w-5xl mx-auto">
      <header className="mb-3 md:mb-6 animate-fadeIn">

        open={open}

            <br />

=== PLIK: C:\Users\AdamPilny\Desktop\talent\Aplikacja-trenera-pi-karskiego-main\app\(app)\kalendarz\nowy\actions.ts ===
'use server';

import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { getActiveDruzynaId } from '@/lib/druzyny';
import { enforceRateLimit } from '@/lib/rate-limit';
import { dodajTreningSchema } from '@/lib/schemas';
import { getErrorMessage } from '@/lib/errors';
import { revalidateKalendarz } from '@/lib/action-result';
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';

function toISODate(d: Date): string {
  const y = d.getFullYear();
  const m = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  const dd = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${y}-${m}-${dd}`;
}

export async function dodajTrening(formData: FormData) {
  const supabase = await createClient();

  const druzynaId = await getActiveDruzynaId();
  if (!druzynaId) {
    throw new Error('Brak aktywnej druĹĽyny.');
  }

  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (user) {
    try {
      await enforceRateLimit(`user:${user.id}`, 'dodajTrening', 60, 3600);
    } catch (err) {
      throw new Error(getErrorMessage(err));
    }
  }

  const raw: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const [key, value] of formData.entries()) {
    if (typeof value === 'string') raw[key] = value;
  }

  const parsed = dodajTreningSchema.safeParse(raw);

  if (!parsed.success) {
    throw new Error(parsed.error.issues[0].message);
  }

  const {
    data,
    godzina,
    miejsce,
    typ,
    przeciwnik,
    notatki,
    cykl,
    data_do: dataDo,
  } = parsed.data;

  const cyklDostepny = typ === 'trening' && cykl !== 'nie_powtarzaj';
  const dates: string[] = [];

  if (!cyklDostepny) {
    dates.push(data);
  } else {
    if (!dataDo) {
      throw new Error('Wybierz datÄ™ koĹ„cowÄ….');
    }

    const startDate = new Date(data + 'T00:00:00');
    const endDate = new Date(dataDo + 'T00:00:00');

    if (endDate < startDate) {
      throw new Error('Data koĹ„cowa musi byÄ‡ po dacie startu.');
    }

    const stepDays = cykl === 'co_2_tygodnie' ? 14 : 7;
    const diffDays = Math.floor(
      (endDate.getTime() - startDate.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)
    );
    const liczba = Math.floor(diffDays / stepDays) + 1;

    if (liczba < 2) {
      throw new Error('Data koĹ„cowa daje tylko 1 wystÄ…pienie.');
    }
    if (liczba > 52) {
      throw new Error('Maksymalna liczba wystÄ…pieĹ„ to 52 (rok).');
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < liczba; i++) {
      const d = new Date(startDate);
      d.setDate(d.getDate() + i * stepDays);
      dates.push(toISODate(d));
    }
  }

  const cyklId = cyklDostepny ? crypto.randomUUID() : null;

  const rows = dates.map((d) => ({
    data: d,
    godzina,
    miejsce,
    typ,
    przeciwnik,
    notatki,
    cykl_id: cyklId,
    druzyna_id: druzynaId,
  }));

  const { error } = await supabase.from('treningi').insert(rows);

  if (error) {
    throw new Error(getErrorMessage(error));
  }

  revalidateKalendarz();
  redirect('/kalendarz?toast=trening_dodany');
}

export function NowyTreningForm({ defaultData }: { defaultData?: string }) {

    : null;

    const endDate = new Date(dataDo + 'T00:00:00');

      return 'Wybierz poprawnÄ… datÄ™.';

    if (endDate < startDate) {

      return `Zbyt duĹĽo wystÄ…pieĹ„ (${liczba}). SkrĂłÄ‡ zakres do maksymalnie roku.`;
    }

    const format = (d: Date) =>
      d.toLocaleDateString('pl-PL', {
        day: '2-digit',
        month: 'long',
        year: 'numeric',
      });

    const slowo =
      liczba === 1 ? 'wydarzenie' : liczba < 5 ? 'wydarzenia' : 'wydarzeĹ„';

      startDate
    )} do ${format(endDate)}.`;
  }, [cyklDostepny, cykl, data, dataDo]);

import Link from 'next/link';
import { NowyTreningForm } from './NowyTreningForm';
import { ArrowLeft, Plus } from 'lucide-react';

export default async function NowyTrening({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ data?: string }>;
}) {
  const params = await searchParams;
  const defaultData =
    params.data && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(params.data) ? params.data : undefined;

  return (
    <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto">
      <header className="mb-8 flex items-center gap-3 animate-fadeIn">
        <Link
          href="/kalendarz"
          className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/70 hover:bg-slate-900 backdrop-blur-md border border-white/10 flex items-center justify-center text-white transition-colors"
        >
          <ArrowLeft className="w-5 h-5" />
        </Link>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="relative">
            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-br from-green-400 to-green-700 rounded-2xl blur-lg opacity-60" />
            <div className="relative w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-green-400 via-green-500 to-green-700 flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg">
              <Plus className="w-5 h-5 text-white" strokeWidth={2.5} />
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-xl font-bold leading-tight text-white drop-shadow-lg">
              Nowe wydarzenie
            </h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-white/70 font-medium mt-0.5 drop-shadow">
              Trening, mecz, sparing lub turniej
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      <NowyTreningForm defaultData={defaultData} />
    </div>
  );
}

'use server';

import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { getActiveDruzynaId } from '@/lib/druzyny';
import { enforceRateLimit, checkRateLimit } from '@/lib/rate-limit';
import { aktualizujTreningSchema } from '@/lib/schemas';
import { getErrorMessage } from '@/lib/errors';
import { revalidateKalendarz } from '@/lib/action-result';

export async function aktualizujTrening(
  id: number,
  scope: 'single' | 'all',
  formData: FormData
): Promise<{ success: true; scope: 'single' | 'all' }> {
  const supabase = await createClient();

  const druzynaId = await getActiveDruzynaId();
  if (!druzynaId) {
    throw new Error('Brak aktywnej druzyny.');
  }

  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (user) {
    await enforceRateLimit(`user:${user.id}`, 'aktualizujTrening', 100, 3600);
  }

  const raw: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const [key, value] of formData.entries()) {
    if (typeof value === 'string') raw[key] = value;
  }

  const parsed = aktualizujTreningSchema.safeParse(raw);

  if (!parsed.success) {
    throw new Error(parsed.error.issues[0].message);
  }

  const { data: trening, error: errFetch } = await supabase
    .from('treningi')
    .select('id, cykl_id')
    .eq('id', id)
    .eq('druzyna_id', druzynaId)
    .single();

  if (errFetch || !trening) {
    throw new Error('Nie znaleziono treningu w aktywnej druzynie.');
  }

  const { data, godzina, miejsce, typ, przeciwnik, notatki } = parsed.data;

  if (scope === 'single' || !trening.cykl_id) {
    const { error } = await supabase
      .from('treningi')
      .update({ data, godzina, miejsce, typ, przeciwnik, notatki })
      .eq('id', id)
      .eq('druzyna_id', druzynaId);

    if (error) throw new Error(getErrorMessage(error));
  } else {
    const { error } = await supabase
      .from('treningi')
      .update({ godzina, miejsce, typ, przeciwnik, notatki })
      .eq('cykl_id', trening.cykl_id)
      .eq('druzyna_id', druzynaId);

    if (error) throw new Error(getErrorMessage(error));
  }

  revalidateKalendarz();
  return { success: true, scope: trening.cykl_id && scope === 'all' ? 'all' : 'single' };
}

export async function usunTreningZakres(
  id: number,
  scope: 'single' | 'all'
): Promise<{ success: true; count: number } | { error: string }> {
  if (!id || isNaN(id)) {
    return { error: 'Nieprawidlowe ID.' };
  }

  const supabase = await createClient();
  const druzynaId = await getActiveDruzynaId();
  if (!druzynaId) return { error: 'Brak aktywnej druzyny.' };

  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (user) {
    const rl = await checkRateLimit(`user:${user.id}`, 'usunTreningZakres', 50, 3600);
    if (!rl.allowed) {
      return { error: `Za duzo operacji. Sprobuj ponownie za ${Math.ceil(rl.retryAfter / 60)} min.` };
    }
  }

  const { data: trening, error: errFetch } = await supabase
    .from('treningi')
    .select('id, cykl_id')
    .eq('id', id)
    .eq('druzyna_id', druzynaId)
    .single();

  if (errFetch || !trening) {
    return { error: 'Nie znaleziono treningu.' };
  }

  let count = 0;

  if (scope === 'all' && trening.cykl_id) {
    const { error, count: deletedCount } = await supabase
      .from('treningi')
      .delete({ count: 'exact' })
      .eq('cykl_id', trening.cykl_id)
      .eq('druzyna_id', druzynaId);
    if (error) return { error: getErrorMessage(error) };
    count = deletedCount ?? 0;
  } else {
    const { error, count: deletedCount } = await supabase
      .from('treningi')
      .delete({ count: 'exact' })
      .eq('id', id)
      .eq('druzyna_id', druzynaId);
    if (error) return { error: getErrorMessage(error) };
    count = deletedCount ?? 0;
  }

  revalidateKalendarz();
  return { success: true, count };
}

import Link from 'next/link';
import { notFound, redirect } from 'next/navigation';

import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { getActiveDruzynaId } from '@/lib/druzyny';
import { EdytujForm } from './EdytujForm';
import { ArrowLeft, Pencil } from 'lucide-react';

export default async function EdytujTrening({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
}) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const idNum = parseInt(id, 10);
  if (isNaN(idNum)) notFound();

  const supabase = await createClient();
  const druzynaId = await getActiveDruzynaId();

  // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
  const { data: trening, error } = await supabase
    .from('treningi')
    .select('id, data, godzina, typ, przeciwnik, miejsce, notatki, cykl_id')
    .eq('id', idNum)

=== PLIK: C:\Users\AdamPilny\Desktop\talent\Aplikacja-trenera-pi-karskiego-main\app\(app)\kalendarz\[id]\obecnosc\actions.ts ===
'use server';

import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { getActiveDruzynaId } from '@/lib/druzyny';
import { enforceRateLimit } from '@/lib/rate-limit';
import { isDemoMode } from '@/lib/demo';
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';
import { revalidatePath } from 'next/cache';

const DOZWOLONE_STATUSY = new Set<string>([
  'obecny',
  'nieobecny',
  'spozniony',
  'usprawiedliwiony',
]);

export async function zapiszObecnosc(treningId: number, formData: FormData) {
  if (await isDemoMode()) {
    throw new Error('W wersji demo nie zapisujemy zmian. ZaĹ‚ĂłĹĽ konto, aby korzystaÄ‡ z aplikacji.');
  }

  if (!Number.isInteger(treningId) || treningId <= 0) {
    throw new Error('NieprawidĹ‚owe ID treningu.');
  }

  const supabase = await createClient();

  const druzynaId = await getActiveDruzynaId();
  if (!druzynaId) {
    throw new Error('Brak aktywnej druĹĽyny.');
  }

  const {
    data: { user },
  } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (user) {
    await enforceRateLimit(`user:${user.id}`, 'zapiszObecnosc', 200, 3600);
  }

  const { data: trening, error: errT } = await supabase
    .from('treningi')
    .select('id')
    .eq('id', treningId)
    .eq('druzyna_id', druzynaId)
    .single();

  if (errT || !trening) {
    throw new Error('Nie znaleziono treningu w aktywnej druĹĽynie.');
  }

  const { data: zawodnicyDruzyny } = await supabase
    .from('zawodnicy')
    .select('id')
    .eq('druzyna_id', druzynaId);

  const dozwoleniIds = new Set((zawodnicyDruzyny ?? []).map((z) => z.id));

  const updates: {
    trening_id: number;
    zawodnik_id: number;
    status: string;
  }[] = [];

  let odrzucone = 0;

  for (const [key, value] of formData.entries()) {
    if (!key.startsWith('status_')) continue;

    const zawodnikId = Number(key.slice('status_'.length));
    if (!Number.isInteger(zawodnikId) || zawodnikId <= 0) {
      odrzucone++;
      continue;
    }
    if (!dozwoleniIds.has(zawodnikId)) {
      odrzucone++;
      continue;
    }
    if (typeof value !== 'string' || !DOZWOLONE_STATUSY.has(value)) {
      odrzucone++;
      continue;
    }

    updates.push({
      trening_id: treningId,
      zawodnik_id: zawodnikId,
      status: value,
    });
  }

  if (updates.length === 0) {
    if (odrzucone > 0) {
      throw new Error('NieprawidĹ‚owe dane obecnoĹ›ci.');
    }
    redirect('/kalendarz');
  }

  const { error } = await supabase
    .from('obecnosc')
    .upsert(updates, { onConflict: 'trening_id,zawodnik_id' });

  if (error) {
    throw new Error(error.message);
  }

  revalidatePath('/kalendarz');
  revalidatePath(`/kalendarz/${treningId}/obecnosc`);
  revalidatePath('/dashboard');
  revalidatePath('/statystyki');
  redirect('/kalendarz?toast=obecnosc_zapisana');
}

        init[z.id] = 'nieobecny';
        return;

        init[z.id] = 'nieobecny';
        return;

      init[z.id] = 'obecny';

    return init;

      else if (v === 'nieobecny') s.nieobecny += 1;
    });
    return s;

      return;

    `${imie.charAt(0)}${nazwisko.charAt(0)}`.toUpperCase();

}) {

        </div>

          treningId={idNum}
          zawodnicy={zawodnicy}
          initialStatus={initialStatus}
          previousStatus={previousStatus}
          typTreningu={trening.typ}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

=== PLIK: C:\Users\AdamPilny\Desktop\talent\Aplikacja-trenera-pi-karskiego-main\app\(app)\kalendarz\actions.ts ===
'use server';

import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { getActiveDruzynaId } from '@/lib/druzyny';
import { enforceRateLimit } from '@/lib/rate-limit';
import { revalidateKalendarz } from '@/lib/action-result';

export async function przeniesTrening(
  id: number,
  newDate: string
): Promise<{ error?: string; success?: boolean }> {
  if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
    return { error: 'NieprawidĹ‚owe ID treningu.' };
  }

  if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(newDate)) {
    return { error: 'NieprawidĹ‚owy format daty.' };
  }
  const [y, m, d] = newDate.split('-').map(Number);
  const roundTrip = new Date(y, m - 1, d);
  if (
    roundTrip.getFullYear() !== y ||
    roundTrip.getMonth() !== m - 1 ||
    roundTrip.getDate() !== d
  ) {
    return { error: 'NieprawidĹ‚owa data.' };
  }

  const supabase = await createClient();
  const druzynaId = await getActiveDruzynaId();
  if (!druzynaId) return { error: 'Brak aktywnej druĹĽyny.' };

  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (user) {
    try {
      await enforceRateLimit(`user:${user.id}`, 'przeniesTrening', 100, 3600);
    } catch (err) {
      return { error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Za duĹĽo operacji.' };
    }
  }

  const { data: updated, error } = await supabase
    .from('treningi')
    .update({ data: newDate })
    .eq('id', id)
    .eq('druzyna_id', druzynaId)
    .select('id')
    .maybeSingle();

  if (error) return { error: error.message };
  if (!updated) return { error: 'Nie znaleziono treningu.' };

  revalidateKalendarz();
  return { success: true };
}

'use client';

import { useState, useTransition } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { Trash2, Calendar, Repeat } from 'lucide-react';
import { usunTreningZakres } from './[id]/edytuj/actions';
import { ConfirmDialog } from '@/components/ConfirmDialog';

export function DeleteEventButton({
  id,
  cyklId,
  size = 'sm',
  variant = 'icon',
}: {
  id: number;
  cyklId?: string | null;
  size?: 'sm' | 'md';
  variant?: 'icon' | 'tile';
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [scope, setScope] = useState<'single' | 'all'>('single');
  const [isPending, startTransition] = useTransition();
  const router = useRouter();
  const isCyclic = !!cyklId;

  // Subtelna czerwieĹ„ domyĹ›lnie â€” nawet bez hover.
  const btnClass =
    variant === 'tile'
      ? 'inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-red-500/5 hover:bg-red-500/15 border border-red-400/25 hover:border-red-400/50 text-red-300/80 hover:text-red-200 text-xs font-semibold transition-all disabled:opacity-50'
      : size === 'md'
      ? 'w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-red-500/5 hover:bg-red-500/20 border border-red-400/20 hover:border-red-400/50 flex items-center justify-center text-red-400/60 hover:text-red-300 transition-all disabled:opacity-50'
      : 'w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-red-500/5 hover:bg-red-500/20 border border-red-400/20 hover:border-red-400/50 flex items-center justify-center text-red-400/60 hover:text-red-300 transition-all disabled:opacity-50';

  const iconClass =
    variant === 'tile' ? 'w-3.5 h-3.5' : size === 'md' ? 'w-4 h-4' : 'w-3.5 h-3.5';

  const handleDelete = () => {
    const finalScope = isCyclic ? scope : 'single';
    setOpen(false);
    startTransition(async () => {
      const result = await usunTreningZakres(id, finalScope);

      if ('error' in result) {
        toast.error('Nie udaĹ‚o siÄ™ usunÄ…Ä‡', {
          description: result.error,
        });
        return;
      }

      if (finalScope === 'all' && result.count > 1) {
        toast.success('CaĹ‚a seria usuniÄ™ta', {
          description: `UsuniÄ™to ${result.count} treningĂłw z cyklu`,
        });
      } else {
        toast.success('Trening usuniÄ™ty');
      }

      router.refresh();
    });
  };

  return (
    <>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={(e) => {
          e.preventDefault();
          e.stopPropagation();
          setOpen(true);
        }}
        disabled={isPending}
        className={btnClass}
        title="UsuĹ„ wydarzenie"
        aria-label="UsuĹ„ wydarzenie"
      >
        <Trash2 className={iconClass} />
        {variant === 'tile' && <span className="hidden md:inline">UsuĹ„</span>}
      </button>

      <ConfirmDialog
        open={open}
        title={isCyclic ? 'UsunÄ…Ä‡ z serii?' : 'UsunÄ…Ä‡ to wydarzenie?'}
        variant="danger"
        confirmLabel={
          isPending ? 'Usuwanie...' : isCyclic && scope === 'all' ? 'UsuĹ„ seriÄ™' : 'UsuĹ„'
        }
        loading={isPending}
        onCancel={() => setOpen(false)}
        onConfirm={handleDelete}
        message={
          isCyclic ? (
            <div className="space-y-3">
              <p className="text-sm">
                Ten trening jest czÄ™Ĺ›ciÄ… <span className="font-bold text-white">cyklu</span>. Co
                chcesz usunÄ…Ä‡?
              </p>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 mt-3">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setScope('single')}
                  className={`p-3 rounded-xl border text-left transition-all ${
                    scope === 'single'
                      ? 'bg-blue-500/15 border-blue-400/60 ring-1 ring-blue-400/30'
                      : 'bg-white/5 border-white/10 hover:border-white/30'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-1">
                    <Calendar className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-blue-400" />

                  </div>
                  <div className="text-[10px] text-white/60 leading-tight">

                  </div>
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setScope('all')}
                  className={`p-3 rounded-xl border text-left transition-all ${
                    scope === 'all'
                      ? 'bg-purple-500/15 border-purple-400/60 ring-1 ring-purple-400/30'
                      : 'bg-white/5 border-white/10 hover:border-white/30'

  ChevronRight,
} from 'lucide-react';

// gdy uĹĽytkownik ma wydarzenia w danym dniu.

export function EventCard({
  trening,
  stats,
  onDragStart,
  isDragging,
  highlighted = false,
}: {
  trening: Trening;
  stats: StatsMap;
  onDragStart?: (e: React.DragEvent, id: number) => void;
  isDragging?: boolean;
  highlighted?: boolean;
}) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const cfg = TYP_CONFIG[trening.typ] ?? TYP_CONFIG.trening;
  const s = stats[trening.id];
  const proc = s && s.total > 0 ? Math.round((s.obecni / s.total) * 100) : 0;

  const mapsUrl = trening.miejsce?.trim()
    ? `https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=${encodeURIComponent(
        trening.miejsce.trim()
      )}`
    : null;

  const goToAttendance = () => {
    router.push(`/kalendarz/${trening.id}/obecnosc`);
  };

  return (
    <div
      className={`group relative rounded-xl transition-all ${
        isDragging ? 'opacity-50' : ''
      } ${
        highlighted
          ? 'bg-green-500/[0.06] border-[1.5px] border-green-400/55 shadow-[0_0_12px_rgba(34,197,94,0.25)]'
          : 'bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 border border-white/5 hover:border-white/20'
      }`}
    >
      <div
        onClick={goToAttendance}
        draggable={!!onDragStart}
        onDragStart={(e) => onDragStart?.(e, trening.id)}
        className="block p-2.5 cursor-pointer"
      >
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <div
            className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br ${cfg.gradient} flex items-center justify-center shadow-md text-white text-sm font-black flex-shrink-0`}
          >
            {cfg.icon}
          </div>
          <div className="flex-shrink-0 w-12">
            <div className="text-sm font-bold text-white">{trening.godzina}</div>
          </div>
          <div className="w-px h-8 bg-white/10" />
          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 pr-24">
            <div className="font-semibold text-white text-xs truncate">
              {cfg.label}
              {trening.przeciwnik && (
                <span className="text-white/60 font-medium"> vs {trening.przeciwnik}</span>
              )}
            </div>
            {trening.miejsce ? (
              mapsUrl ? (
                <a
                  href={mapsUrl}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  onClick={(e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                  }}
                  draggable={false}
                  onDragStart={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
                  title="Otworz w Google Maps"
                  className="text-[10px] text-white/50 hover:text-green-400 mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-1 group/maps transition-colors w-fit max-w-full"
                >
                  <MapPin className="w-2.5 h-2.5 flex-shrink-0" />

                </a>
              ) : (
                <div className="text-[10px] text-white/50 mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-1">
                  <MapPin className="w-2.5 h-2.5 flex-shrink-0" />
                  <span className="truncate">{trening.miejsce}</span>
                </div>
              )
            ) : (
              <div className="text-[10px] text-white/50 mt-0.5">Brak lokalizacji</div>
            )}
            {s && s.total > 0 ? (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-1">
                <span className="text-[10px] text-white/60">

        <div className="pointer-events-auto">
          <Link
            href={`/kalendarz/${trening.id}/edytuj`}
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
            className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/15 border border-white/10 hover:border-white/20 flex items-center justify-center text-white/40 hover:text-white transition-all"
            title="Edytuj wydarzenie"
          >
            <Pencil className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </Link>
        </div>
        <div className="pointer-events-auto">
          <DeleteEventButton id={trening.id} cyklId={trening.cykl_id} />
        </div>
        <ChevronRight
          className={`w-4 h-4 transition-all flex-shrink-0 ${
            highlighted ? 'text-green-400' : 'text-white/40'
          }`}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useState, useMemo, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import dynamic from 'next/dynamic';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import {
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  Plus,
  Calendar as CalendarIcon,
  Pencil,
  Trash2,
  Trophy,
  Flame,
  Target,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { przeniesTrening } from './actions';
import { CompactStat } from './CompactStat';
import { PanelMiesiaca } from './PanelMiesiaca';
import { UpcomingRow } from './UpcomingRow';
import {
  TYP_CONFIG,
  MONTHS_PL,
  MONTHS_PL_GENITIVE,
  DAYS_SHORT,
  DAYS_LONG,
  HOLIDAYS,
} from './constants';
import {
  pad2,
  toISO,
  getLocalISO,
  parseISO,
  daysInMonth,
  firstWeekdayMon,
  formatShort,
  getMonday,
} from './utils';
import { PageHeaderLogo } from '@/components/PageHeaderLogo';
import { EventCard } from '@/components/EventCard';
import type { Trening, StatsMap } from './types';

const DeleteEventButton = dynamic(
  () => import('./DeleteEventButton').then((m) => m.DeleteEventButton),
  {
    ssr: false,
    loading: () => (
      <button
        type="button"
        disabled
        className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-red-500/5 border border-red-400/20 text-red-400/30 text-xs font-semibold"
      >
        <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
        <span className="hidden md:inline">UsuĹ„</span>
      </button>
    ),
  }
);

type Props = {
  treningi: Trening[];
  stats: StatsMap;
  topBar: React.ReactNode;
  logoUrl: string | null;
};

function liczbaSlowo(n: number): string {
  if (n === 1) return 'wydarzenie';
  if (n >= 2 && n <= 4) return 'wydarzenia';
  return 'wydarzeĹ„';
}

export function KalendarzClient({ treningi, stats, topBar, logoUrl }: Props) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const todayISO = useMemo(() => getLocalISO(), []);
  const todayDate = useMemo(() => parseISO(todayISO), [todayISO]);

  const [viewYear, setViewYear] = useState(todayDate.getFullYear());
  const [viewMonth, setViewMonth] = useState(todayDate.getMonth());
  const [selectedDate, setSelectedDate] = useState(todayISO);
  const [toast, setToast] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [draggedId, setDraggedId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [dragOverDate, setDragOverDate] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [flashKey, setFlashKey] = useState(0);

  const calendarRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const touchStartX = useRef<number>(0);

  const eventsByDate = useMemo(() => {
    const map: Record<string, Trening[]> = {};
    treningi.forEach((t) => {
      if (!map[t.data]) map[t.data] = [];
      map[t.data].push(t);
    });
    Object.values(map).forEach((arr) =>
      arr.sort((a, b) => a.godzina.localeCompare(b.godzina))
    );
    return map;
  }, [treningi]);

  const monthPrefix = `${viewYear}-${pad2(viewMonth + 1)}`;
  const monthStats = useMemo(() => {
    const inMonth = treningi.filter((t) => t.data.startsWith(monthPrefix));
    const liczbaTypu = (typ: string) => inMonth.filter((t) => t.typ === typ).length;
    return {
      treningi: liczbaTypu('trening'),
      mecze: liczbaTypu('mecz'),
      sparingi: liczbaTypu('sparing'),
      turnieje: liczbaTypu('turniej'),
      total: inMonth.length,
    };
  }, [treningi, monthPrefix]);

  const selectedEvents = eventsByDate[selectedDate] ?? [];
  const isTodaySelected = selectedDate === todayISO;

  const upcoming = useMemo(() => {
    return treningi
      .filter((t) => t.data >= todayISO)
      .sort((a, b) => {
        const cmp = a.data.localeCompare(b.data);
        if (cmp !== 0) return cmp;
        return a.godzina.localeCompare(b.godzina);
      })
      .slice(0, 3);
  }, [treningi, todayISO]);

  const changeMonth = (delta: number) => {
    let m = viewMonth + delta;
    let y = viewYear;
    if (m > 11) {
      m = 0;
      y++;
    }
    if (m < 0) {
      m = 11;
      y--;
    }
    setViewMonth(m);
    setViewYear(y);
  };

  const selectDay = (iso: string) => {
    if (iso === selectedDate) return;
    setSelectedDate(iso);
    setFlashKey((k) => k + 1);
  };

  const showToast = (msg: string) => {
    setToast(msg);
    setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 2500);
  };

  const handleFabClick = () => {
    router.push(`/kalendarz/nowy?data=${selectedDate}`);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const el = calendarRef.current;
    if (!el) return;
    const onTouchStart = (e: TouchEvent) => {
      touchStartX.current = e.touches[0].clientX;
    };
    const onTouchEnd = (e: TouchEvent) => {
      const delta = e.changedTouches[0].clientX - touchStartX.current;
      if (Math.abs(delta) > 60) changeMonth(delta > 0 ? -1 : 1);
    };
    el.addEventListener('touchstart', onTouchStart, { passive: true });
    el.addEventListener('touchend', onTouchEnd, { passive: true });
    return () => {
      el.removeEventListener('touchstart', onTouchStart);
      el.removeEventListener('touchend', onTouchEnd);
    };
  }, [viewMonth, viewYear]);

  const handleDragStart = (e: React.DragEvent, id: number) => {
    setDraggedId(id);
    e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';
    e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', String(id));
  };

  const handleDragOver = (e: React.DragEvent, iso: string) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (dragOverDate !== iso) setDragOverDate(iso);
  };

  const handleDragLeave = () => {
    setDragOverDate(null);
  };

  const handleDrop = async (e: React.DragEvent, iso: string) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setDragOverDate(null);
    if (draggedId == null) return;
    const id = draggedId;
    setDraggedId(null);
    const result = await przeniesTrening(id, iso);
    if (result?.error) showToast(`BĹ‚Ä…d: ${result.error}`);
    else {
      showToast(`Przeniesiono na ${formatShort(iso)}`);
      setSelectedDate(iso);
      router.refresh();
    }
  };

  const renderDayCell = (
    dayNum: number,
    otherMonth: boolean,
    iso: string | null
  ) => {
    if (otherMonth || !iso) {
      return (
        <div className="aspect-square lg:aspect-auto lg:h-16 rounded-lg border border-transparent flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-1 opacity-30">
          <div className="text-xs lg:text-sm text-white/40">{dayNum}</div>
        </div>
      );
    }
    const dateObj = parseISO(iso);
    const dayOfWeek = dateObj.getDay();
    const isToday = iso === todayISO;
    const isSelected = iso === selectedDate;
    const isWeekend = dayOfWeek === 0 || dayOfWeek === 6;
    const isHoliday = HOLIDAYS.has(iso);
    const isDragOver = dragOverDate === iso;
    const events = eventsByDate[iso] ?? [];

    const classes = [
      'aspect-square lg:aspect-auto lg:h-16 rounded-lg border flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-1 relative cursor-pointer transition-all select-none',
      isToday ? 'day-today' : '',
      isSelected && !isToday ? 'day-selected' : '',
      isWeekend && !isToday && !isSelected ? 'day-weekend' : '',
      isHoliday && !isToday && !isSelected ? 'day-holiday' : '',
      isDragOver ? 'bg-green-500/20 border-green-400/60' : '',
      !isToday && !isSelected ? 'border-transparent day-hoverable' : '',
    ]
      .filter(Boolean)
      .join(' ');

    return (
      <div
        onClick={() => selectDay(iso)}
        onDragOver={(e) => handleDragOver(e, iso)}
        onDragLeave={handleDragLeave}
        onDrop={(e) => handleDrop(e, iso)}
        className={classes}
      >
        <div
          className={`text-xs lg:text-lg day-number ${
            isToday ? 'font-bold text-green-300' : 'text-white/80'
          }`}
        >
          {dayNum}
        </div>
        {isToday && (
          <div className="text-[7px] lg:text-[9px] day-label uppercase tracking-wider leading-none mt-0.5 hidden lg:block">
            DziĹ›
          </div>
        )}
        {events.length > 0 && (
          <div className="flex gap-0.5 lg:gap-1 mt-0.5 lg:mt-1 items-center">
            {events.slice(0, 3).map((e, i) => (
              <span
                key={i}
                className={`w-1 h-1 lg:w-1.5 lg:h-1.5 rounded-full ${
                  TYP_CONFIG[e.typ]?.color ?? 'bg-slate-500'
                }`}
              />
            ))}
            {events.length > 3 && (
              <span
                className={`text-[7px] lg:text-[9px] font-bold ml-0.5 ${
                  isToday ? 'text-white' : 'text-white/60'
                }`}
              >
                +{events.length - 3}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  };

  const renderMonthGrid = () => {
    const cells: React.ReactNode[] = [];
    const firstDay = firstWeekdayMon(viewYear, viewMonth);
    const totalDays = daysInMonth(viewYear, viewMonth);
    const prevMonthDays = daysInMonth(viewYear, viewMonth - 1);

    for (let i = firstDay - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const dayNum = prevMonthDays - i;
      cells.push(
        <div key={`prev-${dayNum}`}>{renderDayCell(dayNum, true, null)}</div>
      );
    }

    for (let d = 1; d <= totalDays; d++) {
      const iso = toISO(viewYear, viewMonth, d);
      cells.push(<div key={iso}>{renderDayCell(d, false, iso)}</div>);
    }

    const remaining = (7 - (cells.length % 7)) % 7;
    for (let d = 1; d <= remaining; d++) {
      cells.push(<div key={`next-${d}`}>{renderDayCell(d, true, null)}</div>);
    }

    return cells;
  };

  const selectedDateObj = parseISO(selectedDate);
  const selectedDayLabel = DAYS_LONG[selectedDateObj.getDay()];
  const selectedMonthLabel = MONTHS_PL_GENITIVE[selectedDateObj.getMonth()];

  const liczbaWydarzen = selectedEvents.length;

  const lewyObszar = (

          }`}
        />
        <div
          className={`relative w-12 h-12 lg:w-11 lg:h-11 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg ${
            isTodaySelected
              ? 'bg-gradient-to-br from-green-400 via-green-500 to-green-700'
              : 'bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-400 via-blue-500 to-blue-700'
          }`}
        >
          <span className="text-white font-black text-lg">

      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
        <div className="flex items-baseline gap-2 mb-1 lg:mb-0.5 flex-wrap">

              isTodaySelected ? 'text-green-400' : 'text-blue-400'

'use client';

import { Plus } from 'lucide-react';
import { EventCard } from './EventCard';
import { DAYS_LONG, MONTHS_PL_GENITIVE } from './constants';
import { pad2, parseISO } from './utils';
import type { Trening, StatsMap } from './types';

export function ListaDnia({
  selectedDate,
  todayISO,
  events,
  stats,
  onAddClick,
  onDragStart,
  draggedId,
}: {
  selectedDate: string;
  todayISO: string;
  events: Trening[];
  stats: StatsMap;
  onAddClick: () => void;
  onDragStart: (e: React.DragEvent, id: number) => void;
  draggedId: number | null;
}) {

  const isToday = selectedDate === todayISO;
  const monthGenitive = MONTHS_PL_GENITIVE[dateObj.getMonth()];

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-900/80 backdrop-blur-md border border-white/10 rounded-3xl p-5 lg:p-5 animate-fadeIn shadow-2xl shadow-black/60">

          <div className="text-2xl lg:text-3xl font-black text-white">
            {pad2(dateObj.getDate())}
          </div>
          <div>

              {DAYS_LONG[dateObj.getDay()]}
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs lg:text-sm text-white/50 capitalize">
              {monthGenitive} {dateObj.getFullYear()}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

          <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-green-400 bg-green-500/10 border border-green-400/30 rounded-full px-2.5 py-1">
            Dzisiaj
          </span>
        )}
      </div>

        {events.map((t) => (
          <EventCard
            key={t.id}
            trening={t}
            stats={stats}
            onDragStart={onDragStart}
            isDragging={draggedId === t.id}

          />
        ))}
      </div>
      <button
        onClick={onAddClick}
        className="group w-full mt-3 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-dashed border-white/20 hover:border-white/40 text-white/50 hover:text-white text-xs font-semibold transition-all"
      >
        <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5 group-hover:rotate-90 transition-transform" />

      </button>

=== PLIK: C:\Users\AdamPilny\Desktop\talent\Aplikacja-trenera-pi-karskiego-main\app\(app)\kalendarz\page.tsx ===
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';
import { KalendarzClient } from './KalendarzClient';
import { TopBar } from '@/components/TopBar';
import { getActiveDruzynaId, getActiveDruzyna } from '@/lib/druzyny';
import { toISO } from '@/lib/dates';

type Trening = {
  id: number;
  data: string;
  godzina: string;
  typ: string;
  przeciwnik: string | null;
  miejsce: string | null;
  notatki: string | null;
  cykl_id: string | null;
};

type TreningZObecnoscia = Trening & {
  obecnosc: { trening_id: number; status: string }[] | null;
};

type StatsMap = Record<
  number,
  { obecni: number; nieobecni: number; total: number }
>;

export default async function KalendarzPage() {
  const supabase = await createClient();

  const druzynaId = await getActiveDruzynaId();
  if (!druzynaId) redirect('/druzyny/nowa');

  const activeDruzyna = await getActiveDruzyna();

  const dzis = new Date();
  const od = new Date(dzis.getFullYear(), dzis.getMonth() - 2, 1);
  const do_ = new Date(dzis.getFullYear(), dzis.getMonth() + 3, 0);

  const { data: rawTreningi } = await supabase
    .from('treningi')
    .select(`
      id, data, godzina, typ, przeciwnik, miejsce, notatki, cykl_id,
      obecnosc (trening_id, status)
    `)
    .eq('druzyna_id', druzynaId)
    .gte('data', toISO(od))
    .lte('data', toISO(do_))
    .order('data', { ascending: true });

  const lista = (rawTreningi ?? []) as TreningZObecnoscia[];

  const stats: StatsMap = {};
  lista.forEach((t) => {
    let obecni = 0;
    let nieobecni = 0;
    for (const o of t.obecnosc ?? []) {
      if (o.status === 'obecny' || o.status === 'spozniony') obecni++;
      else nieobecni++;
    }
    stats[t.id] = { obecni, nieobecni, total: obecni + nieobecni };
  });

  const treningi: Trening[] = lista.map((t) => ({
    id: t.id,
    data: t.data,
    godzina: t.godzina,
    typ: t.typ,
    przeciwnik: t.przeciwnik,
    miejsce: t.miejsce,
    notatki: t.notatki,
    cykl_id: t.cykl_id,
  }));

  return (
    <KalendarzClient
      treningi={treningi}
      stats={stats}
      topBar={<TopBar />}
      logoUrl={activeDruzyna?.logo_url ?? null}
    />
  );
}

// app/(app)/kalendarz/PanelMiesiaca.tsx
'use client';

import { useMemo } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Plus, TrendingUp, Award } from 'lucide-react';
import type { Trening, StatsMap } from './types';

export function PanelMiesiaca({
  monthStats,
  monthLabel,
  treningi,
  stats,
  monthPrefix,
  onAddClick,
}: {
  monthStats: {
    treningi: number;
    mecze: number;
    sparingi: number;
    turnieje: number;
    total: number;
  };
  monthLabel: string;
  treningi: Trening[];
  stats: StatsMap;
  monthPrefix: string;
  onAddClick: () => void;
}) {
  const frekwencja = useMemo(() => {
    const inMonth = treningi.filter((t) => t.data.startsWith(monthPrefix));
    let obecni = 0;
    let total = 0;
    inMonth.forEach((t) => {
      const s = stats[t.id];
      if (s) {
        obecni += s.obecni;
        total += s.total;
      }
    });
    const procent = total > 0 ? Math.round((obecni / total) * 100) : 0;
    return { obecni, total, procent };
  }, [treningi, stats, monthPrefix]);

  const kolorProcent = (p: number) => {
    if (p >= 80) return { text: 'text-green-400', bg: 'from-green-500 to-green-400' };
    if (p >= 60) return { text: 'text-yellow-400', bg: 'from-yellow-500 to-yellow-400' };
    return { text: 'text-red-400', bg: 'from-red-500 to-red-400' };
  };

  const kol = kolorProcent(frekwencja.procent);

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-900/80 backdrop-blur-md border border-white/10 rounded-3xl p-5 lg:p-5 animate-fadeIn shadow-2xl shadow-black/60">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
        <TrendingUp className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-green-400" />
        <h2 className="text-xs font-bold text-white/70 uppercase tracking-widest">
          {monthLabel}
        </h2>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-5">
        <div className="text-[10px] text-white/40 uppercase tracking-wider font-bold mb-2">
          Frekwencja
        </div>
        {frekwencja.total > 0 ? (
          <>
            <div className="flex items-baseline gap-2 mb-2">
              <span className={`text-4xl font-black leading-none ${kol.text}`}>
                {frekwencja.procent}
              </span>
              <span className="text-lg text-white/40 font-bold">%</span>
            </div>
            <div className="w-full h-2 bg-slate-950/60 rounded-full overflow-hidden mb-2">
              <div
                className={`h-full rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r ${kol.bg}`}
                style={{ width: `${frekwencja.procent}%` }}
              />
            </div>
            <div className="text-[10px] text-white/50">
              {frekwencja.obecni} obecnych z {frekwencja.total} wpisow
            </div>
          </>
        ) : (
          <div className="text-xs text-white/40 py-2">Brak wpisow obecnosci</div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="mb-5 pt-4 border-t border-white/10">
        <div className="text-[10px] text-white/40 uppercase tracking-wider font-bold mb-2">
          Rozklad
        </div>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-blue-500/10 border border-blue-400/20">
            <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-blue-500 flex-shrink-0" />
            <span className="text-xs text-white/70 flex-1">Treningi</span>
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-white">{monthStats.treningi}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-red-500/10 border border-red-400/20">
            <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-red-500 flex-shrink-0" />
            <span className="text-xs text-white/70 flex-1">Mecze</span>
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-white">{monthStats.mecze}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-orange-500/10 border border-orange-400/20">
            <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-orange-500 flex-shrink-0" />
            <span className="text-xs text-white/70 flex-1">Sparingi</span>

=== PLIK: C:\Users\AdamPilny\Desktop\talent\Aplikacja-trenera-pi-karskiego-main\app\(app)\kalendarz\utils.ts ===
// app/(app)/kalendarz/utils.ts
export const pad2 = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');

export const toISO = (y: number, m: number, d: number) =>
  `${y}-${pad2(m + 1)}-${pad2(d)}`;

export const getLocalISO = (date: Date = new Date()) =>
  toISO(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth(), date.getDate());

export const parseISO = (iso: string) => {
  const [y, m, d] = iso.split('-').map(Number);
  return new Date(y, m - 1, d);
};

export const daysInMonth = (y: number, m: number) =>
  new Date(y, m + 1, 0).getDate();

export const firstWeekdayMon = (y: number, m: number) => {
  const d = new Date(y, m, 1).getDay();
  return d === 0 ? 6 : d - 1;
};

export const formatShort = (iso: string) => {
  const d = parseISO(iso);
  return `${pad2(d.getDate())}.${pad2(d.getMonth() + 1)}`;
};

export function getMonday(date: Date) {
  const d = new Date(date);
  const day = d.getDay();
  const diff = d.getDate() - day + (day === 0 ? -6 : 1);
  d.setDate(diff);
  d.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  return d;
}

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import {
  AlertCircle,
  Bug,
  Lightbulb,
  HelpCircle,
  Send,
  Loader2,
  CheckCircle2,
  MessageSquare,
} from 'lucide-react';

const TYPY = [
  { value: 'blad', label: 'ZgĹ‚aszam bĹ‚Ä…d', icon: Bug, color: 'text-red-400' },
  { value: 'pomysl', label: 'PomysĹ‚ na funkcjÄ™', icon: Lightbulb, color: 'text-yellow-400' },
  { value: 'pytanie', label: 'Mam pytanie', icon: HelpCircle, color: 'text-blue-400' },
  { value: 'inne', label: 'Inne', icon: MessageSquare, color: 'text-white/60' },
] as const;

export function PomocForm({ defaultEmail }: { defaultEmail: string }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [typ, setTyp] = useState<'blad' | 'pomysl' | 'pytanie' | 'inne'>('blad');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState(defaultEmail);
  const [temat, setTemat] = useState('');
  const [opis, setOpis] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsLoading(true);
    setError(null);

    if (temat.trim().length < 3) {
      setError('Temat musi mieÄ‡ minimum 3 znaki');
      setIsLoading(false);
      return;
    }
    if (opis.trim().length < 10) {
      setError('Opis musi mieÄ‡ minimum 10 znakĂłw');
      setIsLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    const supabase = createClient();
    const {
      data: { user },
    } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

    const { error } = await supabase.from('zgloszenia').insert({
      user_id: user?.id ?? null,
      user_email: email,
      typ,
      temat: temat.trim(),
      opis: opis.trim(),
      page_url: typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.location.href : null,
      user_agent: typeof navigator !== 'undefined' ? navigator.userAgent : null,
    });

    setIsLoading(false);

    if (error) {
      setError(error.message);
      return;
    }

    setSuccess(true);
  };

  if (success) {
    return (
      <div className="bg-slate-900/80 backdrop-blur-md border border-green-400/30 rounded-3xl p-6 animate-fadeIn shadow-2xl shadow-black/60 text-center">
        <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-green-400 via-green-500 to-green-700 shadow-2xl shadow-green-500/30 mb-4">
          <CheckCircle2 className="w-7 h-7 text-white" strokeWidth={2.5} />
        </div>
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white drop-shadow-lg mb-2">
          DziÄ™kujemy!
        </h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-white/60 mb-6 leading-relaxed">
          Twoje zgĹ‚oszenie zostaĹ‚o zapisane. Odpowiemy najszybciej jak to moĹĽliwe.
        </p>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => router.push('/profil')}
          className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 w-full bg-gradient-to-br from-green-500 to-green-600 hover:from-green-400 hover:to-green-500 text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-2xl transition-all hover:shadow-xl hover:shadow-green-500/50"
        >
          PowrĂłt do profilu
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form
      onSubmit={handleSubmit}
      className="bg-slate-900/80 backdrop-blur-md border border-white/10 rounded-3xl p-6 space-y-5 animate-fadeIn shadow-2xl shadow-black/60"
    >
      <div>
        <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-semibold text-white/70 uppercase tracking-wider mb-3">
          <AlertCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-blue-400" />
          Rodzaj zgĹ‚oszenia
        </label>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
          {TYPY.map((t) => {
            const Icon = t.icon;
            const aktywny = typ === t.value;
            return (
              <button
                key={t.value}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setTyp(t.value)}
                className={`flex items-center gap-2 p-3 rounded-xl border text-left transition-all ${
                  aktywny
                    ? 'bg-blue-500/15 border-blue-400/50 shadow-lg shadow-blue-500/20'
                    : 'bg-white/5 border-white/10 hover:border-white/25'
                }`}
              >
                <Icon className={`w-4 h-4 ${aktywny ? t.color : 'text-white/40'} flex-shrink-0`} />
                <span className={`text-xs font-semibold ${aktywny ? 'text-white' : 'text-white/60'}`}>
                  {t.label}
                </span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="block text-[10px] font-semibold text-white/70 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">
          TwĂłj email
        </label>
        <input
          type="email"
          required
          value={email}
          onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="twoj@email.pl"
          className="w-full bg-white/5 border border-white/15 focus:border-blue-400/60 focus:bg-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white placeholder:text-white/30 outline-none transition-all"
        />
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="block text-[10px] font-semibold text-white/70 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">
          Temat
        </label>
        <input
          type="text"
          required
          value={temat}
          onChange={(e) => setTemat(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="KrĂłtki opis problemu"
          maxLength={100}
          className="w-full bg-white/5 border border-white/15 focus:border-blue-400/60 focus:bg-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white placeholder:text-white/30 outline-none transition-all"
        />
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="block text-[10px] font-semibold text-white/70 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">
          Opis szczegĂłĹ‚owy
        </label>
        <textarea
          required

          onChange={(e) => setOpis(e.target.value)}
          rows={6}
          maxLength={2000}
          placeholder="Opisz dokĹ‚adnie co siÄ™ staĹ‚o. JeĹ›li to bĹ‚Ä…d - napisz co robiĹ‚eĹ›, gdy wystÄ…piĹ‚."
          className="w-full bg-white/5 border border-white/15 focus:border-blue-400/60 focus:bg-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white placeholder:text-white/30 outline-none resize-none transition-all leading-relaxed"
        />
        <div className="text-[10px] text-white/30 text-right mt-1">
          {opis.length} / 2000 znakĂłw
        </div>
      </div>

      {error && (
        <div className="bg-red-500/15 border border-red-400/40 rounded-xl p-3 flex items-start gap-2 text-xs text-red-200">
          <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
          <span>{error}</span>
        </div>
      )}

      <button
        type="submit"
        disabled={isLoading}
        className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-500 to-blue-600 hover:from-blue-400 hover:to-blue-500 text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-2xl transition-all hover:shadow-xl hover:shadow-blue-500/50 hover:-translate-y-0.5 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-wait"

            <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />

          </>
        ) : (

            <Send className="w-4 h-4" />
            WyĹ›lij zgĹ‚oszenie
          </>
        )}
      </button>

      <p className="text-[10px] text-white/40 text-center leading-relaxed">
        ZgĹ‚oszenie trafia bezpoĹ›rednio do twĂłrcy aplikacji. Odpowiemy na podany email.

'use server';

import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { createAdminClient } from '@/lib/supabase/admin';
import { revalidatePath } from 'next/cache';
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { getErrorMessage } from '@/lib/errors';
import { isDemoMode } from '@/lib/demo';

export type ProfilFormState = {
  error?: string;
  success?: boolean;
} | null;

const profilSchema = z.object({
  imie: z.string().trim().max(50, 'ImiÄ™ moĹĽe mieÄ‡ maksymalnie 50 znakĂłw').optional(),
  nazwisko: z.string().trim().max(50, 'Nazwisko moĹĽe mieÄ‡ maksymalnie 50 znakĂłw').optional(),
});

export async function zaktualizujProfil(
  prevState: ProfilFormState,
  formData: FormData
): Promise<ProfilFormState> {
  if (await isDemoMode()) {
    return { error: 'W wersji demo nie zapisujemy zmian. ZaĹ‚ĂłĹĽ konto, aby korzystaÄ‡ z aplikacji.' };
  }

  const supabase = await createClient();

  const raw = {
    imie: formData.get('imie'),
    nazwisko: formData.get('nazwisko'),
  };

  const parsed = profilSchema.safeParse(raw);

  if (!parsed.success) {
    return { error: parsed.error.issues[0].message };
  }

  const { error } = await supabase.auth.updateUser({
    data: {
      imie: parsed.data.imie ?? '',
      nazwisko: parsed.data.nazwisko ?? '',
    },
  });

  if (error) {
    return { error: getErrorMessage(error) };
  }

  revalidatePath('/profil');
  revalidatePath('/dashboard');
  revalidatePath('/kadra');
  revalidatePath('/kalendarz');

  return { success: true };
}

export async function wyloguj() {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  await supabase.auth.signOut();
  redirect('/auth/login');
}

export async function usunKonto(): Promise<{ error?: string }> {
  if (await isDemoMode()) {
    return { error: 'W wersji demo nie moĹĽna usunÄ…Ä‡ konta.' };
  }

  const supabase = await createClient();

  const {
    data: { user },
  } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

  if (!user) {
    redirect('/auth/login');
  }

  const { data: druzyny } = await supabase
    .from('druzyny')
    .select('id')
    .eq('user_id', user.id);

  if (druzyny && druzyny.length > 0) {
    const filesToRemove: string[] = [];
    for (const d of druzyny) {
      const { data: files } = await supabase.storage.from('club-logos').list(d.id);
      if (files && files.length > 0) {
        for (const f of files) {
          filesToRemove.push(`${d.id}/${f.name}`);
        }
      }
    }
    if (filesToRemove.length > 0) {
      await supabase.storage.from('club-logos').remove(filesToRemove);
    }
  }

  const adminClient = createAdminClient();
  const { error } = await adminClient.auth.admin.deleteUser(user.id);

  if (error) {
    return { error: getErrorMessage(error) };
  }

  await supabase.auth.signOut();
  revalidatePath('/');
  revalidatePath('/auth/login');
  redirect('/');
}

=== PLIK: C:\Users\AdamPilny\Desktop\talent\Aplikacja-trenera-pi-karskiego-main\app\(app)\profil\logo-actions.ts ===
'use server';

import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { getActiveDruzynaId } from '@/lib/druzyny';
import { enforceRateLimit } from '@/lib/rate-limit';
import { getErrorMessage } from '@/lib/errors';
import { isDemoMode } from '@/lib/demo';
import { revalidatePath } from 'next/cache';

const ALLOWED_MIMES = ['image/png', 'image/jpeg', 'image/webp'];

export async function uploadLogo(formData: FormData) {
  if (await isDemoMode()) {
    return { error: 'W wersji demo nie zapisujemy zmian. ZaĹ‚ĂłĹĽ konto, aby korzystaÄ‡ z aplikacji.' };
  }

  const supabase = await createClient();
  const druzynaId = await getActiveDruzynaId();

  if (!druzynaId) return { error: 'Brak aktywnej druĹĽyny.' };

  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (user) {
    try {
      await enforceRateLimit(`user:${user.id}`, 'uploadLogo', 10, 3600);
    } catch (err) {
      return { error: getErrorMessage(err) };
    }
  }

  const file = formData.get('logo') as File;
  if (!file || file.size === 0) return { error: 'Nie wybrano pliku.' };

  if (file.size > 2 * 1024 * 1024) {
    return { error: 'Plik jest za duĹĽy (maksymalnie 2 MB).' };
  }

  if (!ALLOWED_MIMES.includes(file.type)) {
    return { error: 'Dozwolone formaty: PNG, JPG, WEBP.' };
  }

  const ext = file.name.split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase() || 'png';
  const filePath = `${druzynaId}/logo.${ext}`;

  try {
    const { data: existingFiles } = await supabase.storage.from('club-logos').list(druzynaId);
    if (existingFiles && existingFiles.length > 0) {
      const pathsToRemove = existingFiles.map((f) => `${druzynaId}/${f.name}`);
      await supabase.storage.from('club-logos').remove(pathsToRemove);
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('BĹ‚Ä…d czyszczenia starego logo:', err);
  }

  const { error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
    .from('club-logos')
    .upload(filePath, file, { upsert: true, contentType: file.type });

  if (uploadError) {
    return { error: getErrorMessage(uploadError) };
  }

  const { data: { publicUrl } } = supabase.storage.from('club-logos').getPublicUrl(filePath);
  const finalUrl = `${publicUrl}?t=${Date.now()}`;

  const { error: dbError } = await supabase
    .from('druzyny')
    .update({ logo_url: finalUrl })
    .eq('id', druzynaId);

  if (dbError) {
    await supabase.storage.from('club-logos').remove([filePath]);
    return { error: getErrorMessage(dbError) };
  }

  revalidatePath('/profil');
  revalidatePath('/dashboard');
  revalidatePath('/druzyny');
  revalidatePath('/', 'layout');

  return { success: true, url: finalUrl };
}

          <span>{state.error}</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {state?.success && (
        <div className="mb-4 bg-green-500/15 border border-green-400/40 rounded-xl p-3 flex items-start gap-2 text-xs text-green-200">

      >
        <Save className="w-4 h-4" />
        Zapisz dane
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
