'use client';

import { AlertCircle, type LucideIcon } from 'lucide-react';

type AuthFieldProps = {
  /** Ikona po lewej stronie labelki */
  icon: LucideIcon;
  /** Etykieta pola (np. "Email", "HasĹ‚o") */
  label: string;
  /** HTML name - wymagane dla autouzupeĹ‚niania i FormData */
  name: string;
  /** HTML id - do powiÄ…zania labelâ†”input (a11y) */
  id: string;
  /** Typ pola: email, password, text, tel */
  type?: 'email' | 'password' | 'text' | 'tel';
  /** WartoĹ›Ä‡ pola (kontrolowany) */
  value: string;
  /** Handler zmiany */
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
  /** Placeholder */
  placeholder?: string;
  /** Komunikat bĹ‚Ä™du (jeĹ›li jest - pole na czerwono) */
  error?: string;
  /** Atrybut autocomplete (email, current-password, new-password, tel) */
  autoComplete?: string;
  /** Opcjonalny element po prawej stronie labelki (np. "ZapomniaĹ‚eĹ›?") */
  labelRight?: React.ReactNode;
};

export function AuthField({
  icon: Icon,
  label,
  name,
  id,
  type = 'text',
  value,
  onChange,
  placeholder,
  error,
  autoComplete,
  labelRight,
}: AuthFieldProps) {
  const errorId = `${id}-error`;
  const hasError = !!error;

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
        <label
          htmlFor={id}
          className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-semibold text-white/70 uppercase tracking-wider"
        >
          <Icon className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-green-400" />
          {label}
        </label>
        {labelRight}
      </div>

      <input
        id={id}
        name={name}
        type={type}
        autoComplete={autoComplete}
        value={value}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
        placeholder={placeholder}
        aria-invalid={hasError}
        aria-describedby={hasError ? errorId : undefined}
        className={`w-full bg-white/5 border rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white placeholder:text-white/30 outline-none transition-all ${
          hasError
            ? 'border-red-400/70 focus:border-red-400 bg-red-500/5'
            : 'border-white/15 focus:border-green-400/60 focus:bg-white/10'
        }`}
      />

      {hasError && (
        <div
          id={errorId}
          role="alert"
          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-2 text-[11px] text-red-300 animate-fadeIn"
        >
          <AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3 flex-shrink-0" />
          <span>{error}</span>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

=== PLIK: C:\Users\AdamPilny\Desktop\talent\Aplikacja-trenera-pi-karskiego-main\components\BackButton.tsx ===
'use client';

import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react';

export function BackButton({ href = '/' }: { href?: string }) {
  const router = useRouter();

  const handleClick = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    // JeĹ›li user przyszedĹ‚ z innej strony w tej samej sesji - wrĂłÄ‡ tam.
    // W przeciwnym razie idĹş do `href` (fallback = "/").
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.history.length > 1) {
      router.back();
    } else {
      router.push(href);
    }
  };

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={handleClick}
      className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-slate-400 hover:text-white mb-8 transition cursor-pointer"
    >
      <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
      PowrĂłt
    </button>
  );
}

'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { usePathname } from 'next/navigation';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import {
  Users,
  Calendar,
  BarChart3,
  Zap,
  User,
  LogOut,
  type LucideIcon,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { ConfirmDialog } from '@/components/ConfirmDialog';

type Item = {
  href: string;
  label: string;
  icon: LucideIcon;
};

const sekcje = [
  {
    nazwa: 'GĹ‚Ăłwne',
    items: [
      { href: '/dashboard', label: 'Dzisiaj', icon: Zap },
      { href: '/kadra', label: 'Kadra', icon: Users },
    ] as Item[],
  },
  {
    nazwa: 'Trening',
    items: [
      { href: '/kalendarz', label: 'Kalendarz', icon: Calendar },
      { href: '/statystyki', label: 'Statystyki', icon: BarChart3 },
    ] as Item[],
  },
  {
    nazwa: 'Konto',
    items: [
      { href: '/profil', label: 'Profil', icon: User },
    ] as Item[],
  },
];

// ĹšcieĹĽki publiczne - bez sidebara i bottom nav
const HIDDEN_PATHS = ['/', '/auth', '/prywatnosc', '/regulamin'];

function shouldHideNav(pathname: string): boolean {
  return HIDDEN_PATHS.some((path) => {
    if (path === '/') return pathname === '/';
    return pathname === path || pathname.startsWith(path + '/');
  });
}

export function BottomNav({
  dzisiajCount,
  userEmail,
  userImie,
  userNazwisko,
}: {
  dzisiajCount: number;
  userEmail: string | null;
  userImie: string | null;
  userNazwisko: string | null;
}) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const [showLogoutModal, setShowLogoutModal] = useState(false);
  const [logoutLoading, setLogoutLoading] = useState(false);

  const hidden = shouldHideNav(pathname);

  async function handleLogout() {
    setLogoutLoading(true);
    const supabase = createClient();
    await supabase.auth.signOut();

    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
    // HARD REDIRECT - peĹ‚ne przeĹ‚adowanie strony.
    //
    // Dlaczego nie router.push + router.refresh:
    //   - router.push nie zabija stanu komponentĂłw klienta
    //   - router.refresh re-renderuje server components, ale klient
    //     trzyma stary stan (np. logoutLoading = true, otwarty modal)
    //
    // window.location.href robi hard reload - wszystkie stany czyszczone.
    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
    window.location.href = '/auth/login';
  }

  if (hidden) {
    return null;
  }

  const pelneImie =
    userImie && userNazwisko ? `${userImie} ${userNazwisko}` : null;
  const inicjal = (userImie?.charAt(0) || userEmail?.charAt(0) || '?').toUpperCase();
  const wszystkieItems = sekcje.flatMap((s) => s.items);

  return (
    <>
      <aside className="hidden md:flex fixed left-0 top-0 h-screen w-60 flex-col z-50 bg-slate-900/95 backdrop-blur-xl border-r border-white/10">
        {/* Logo - przezroczysty wordmark */}
        <div className="p-5 border-b border-white/10">
          <Link href="/dashboard" className="flex items-center">
            <img
              src="/wordmark-transparent-400.png"
              alt="Talent"
              className="h-9 w-auto"
              style={{ mixBlendMode: 'lighten' }}
            />
          </Link>
        </div>

        <nav className="flex-1 p-3 overflow-y-auto">
          {sekcje.map((sekcja, sIdx) => (
            <div key={sekcja.nazwa} className={sIdx > 0 ? 'mt-4' : ''}>
              <div className="text-[10px] text-white/35 uppercase tracking-widest font-bold px-3 py-2">
                {sekcja.nazwa}
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-1">
                {sekcja.items.map((item) => {
                  const aktywny =
                    item.href === '/dashboard'
                      ? pathname === '/dashboard' ||
                        pathname.startsWith('/dashboard/')
                      : pathname.startsWith(item.href);
                  const Icon = item.icon;
                  const showBadge =
                    item.href === '/dashboard' && dzisiajCount > 0;
                  return (
                    <Link
                      key={item.href}
                      href={item.href}
                      className={
                        aktywny
                          ? 'relative flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm bg-green-500/15 text-green-300 font-semibold'
                          : 'relative flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm text-white/60 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/5'
                      }
                    >
                      {aktywny && (
                        <div className="absolute left-0 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-1 h-6 bg-green-400 rounded-r-full" />
                      )}
                      <Icon
                        className="w-5 h-5 flex-shrink-0"
                        strokeWidth={aktywny ? 2.5 : 2}
                      />
                      <span className="flex-1">{item.label}</span>
                      {showBadge && (
                        <span className="bg-green-500 text-white text-[10px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-md min-w-[20px] text-center">
                          {dzisiajCount}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </Link>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </nav>

        <div className="p-3 border-t border-white/10">
          <Link
            href="/profil"
            className="flex items-center gap-3 p-2 rounded-xl bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 mb-2 transition-colors"
          >
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-green-400 to-green-700 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-white text-sm flex-shrink-0">
              {inicjal}
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <div className="text-[10px] text-white/40 uppercase tracking-wider font-bold">
                Zalogowany
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-white truncate">
                {pelneImie ?? userEmail ?? '...'}
              </div>
            </div>
          </Link>
          <button
            onClick={() => setShowLogoutModal(true)}
            className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-red-300 bg-red-500/10 hover:bg-red-500/20 border border-red-400/30 hover:border-red-400/60 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium transition-all"
          >

          {wszystkieItems.map((item) => {
            const aktywny =
              item.href === '/dashboard'
                ? pathname === '/dashboard' ||
                  pathname.startsWith('/dashboard/')
                : pathname.startsWith(item.href);
            const Icon = item.icon;
            return (
              <Link
                key={item.href}
                href={item.href}
                className={`relative flex flex-col items-center justify-center flex-1 py-2 rounded-2xl text-[10px] transition-all ${
                  aktywny
                    ? 'text-green-400'
                    : 'text-white/50 hover:text-white/80'
                }`}
              >
                {aktywny && (
                  <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-green-500/15 rounded-2xl" />

                <Icon
                  className={`relative w-5 h-5 mb-0.5 transition-transform ${
                    aktywny ? 'scale-110' : ''
                  }`}
                  strokeWidth={aktywny ? 2.5 : 2}
                />
                <span
                  className={`relative ${
                    aktywny ? 'font-semibold' : 'font-medium'
                  }`}
                >
                  {item.label}
                </span>
                {aktywny && (
                  <div className="absolute -top-2 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-8 h-1 bg-green-400 rounded-full shadow-lg shadow-green-400/50" />
                )}
              </Link>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </nav>

      <ConfirmDialog
        open={showLogoutModal}
        title="WylogowaÄ‡ siÄ™?"
        message="ZakoĹ„czysz sesjÄ™ i wrĂłcisz do ekranu logowania."
        confirmLabel="Wyloguj"
        variant="warning"
        loading={logoutLoading}
        icon={<LogOut className="w-7 h-7" />}
        onCancel={() => setShowLogoutModal(false)}
        onConfirm={handleLogout}
      />
    </>
  );
}

export function BottomNavSkeleton() {
  return (
    <>
      {/* Desktop sidebar skeleton */}
      <aside
        aria-hidden="true"
        className="hidden md:flex fixed left-0 top-0 h-screen w-60 flex-col z-50 bg-slate-900/95 backdrop-blur-xl border-r border-white/10"
      >

          <div className="h-9 w-32 rounded bg-slate-800/70 animate-pulse" />
        </div>
        <nav className="flex-1 p-3 space-y-2">
          {Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, i) => (
            <div
              key={i}
              className="h-10 rounded-xl bg-slate-800/50 animate-pulse"
              style={{ animationDelay: `${i * 60}ms` }}
            />
          ))}
        </nav>
        <div className="p-3 border-t border-white/10 space-y-2">
          <div className="h-14 rounded-xl bg-slate-800/50 animate-pulse" />
          <div className="h-10 rounded-xl bg-slate-800/50 animate-pulse" />
        </div>
      </aside>

      {/* Mobile bottom nav skeleton */}
      <nav
        aria-hidden="true"
        className="fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 z-50 md:hidden"

'use client';

import { useState, useRef } from 'react';
import { Upload, Image as ImageIcon, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { uploadLogo } from '@/app/(app)/profil/logo-actions';
import { toast } from 'sonner';

export function ClubLogoUpload({ currentLogo }: { currentLogo: string | null }) {
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<string | null>(currentLogo);
  const [isPending, setIsPending] = useState(false);
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const handleFileChange = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;

    setPreview(URL.createObjectURL(file));

    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('logo', file);

    setIsPending(true);
    const result = await uploadLogo(formData);
    setIsPending(false);

    if (result.error) {
      toast.error('BĹ‚Ä…d wgrywania', { description: result.error });
      setPreview(currentLogo);
    } else {
      toast.success('Logo zostaĹ‚o zapisane');
      setPreview(result.url ?? null);
    }

    if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = '';
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
      <div className="relative w-20 h-20 rounded-2xl bg-slate-800 border-2 border-dashed border-white/20 flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden shrink-0">
        {preview ? (
          <img src={preview} alt="Logo klubu" className="w-full h-full object-contain p-1" />
        ) : (
          <ImageIcon className="w-8 h-8 text-white/30" />
        )}
        {isPending && (
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-slate-900/80 flex items-center justify-center">
            <Loader2 className="w-5 h-5 text-green-400 animate-spin" />
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1">
        <input
          type="file"
          accept="image/png, image/jpeg, image/webp"
          className="hidden"
          ref={fileInputRef}
          onChange={handleFileChange}
          disabled={isPending}
        />
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
          disabled={isPending}
          className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 border border-white/10 text-white text-xs font-semibold transition-all disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          <Upload className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          {preview ? 'ZmieĹ„ logo' : 'Wgraj logo'}
        </button>
        <p className="text-[10px] text-white/40 mt-2">
          PNG, JPG lub WEBP. Maks. 2 MB.
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import { AlertTriangle, Info, LogOut, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';

type Variant = 'default' | 'danger' | 'warning' | 'info';

type Props = {
  open: boolean;
  title: string;
  message: React.ReactNode;
  confirmLabel?: string;
  cancelLabel?: string;
  variant?: Variant;
  /** Opcjonalna wĹ‚asna ikona (nadpisuje domyĹ›lnÄ… dla wariantu) */
  icon?: React.ReactNode;
  /** Stan Ĺ‚adowania - wyĹ›wietla spinner, blokuje przyciski */
  loading?: boolean;
  onConfirm: () => void | Promise<void>;
  onCancel: () => void;
};

const VARIANT_STYLES: Record<
  Variant,
  {
    Icon: typeof AlertTriangle;
    iconBg: string;
    iconColor: string;
    button: string;
    buttonShadow: string;
  }
> = {
  default: {
    Icon: Info,
    iconBg: 'bg-green-500/15 border border-green-400/40',
    iconColor: 'text-green-400',
    button: 'from-green-500 to-green-600 hover:from-green-400 hover:to-green-500',
    buttonShadow: 'shadow-green-500/30',
  },
  danger: {
    Icon: AlertTriangle,
    iconBg: 'bg-red-500/15 border border-red-400/40',
    iconColor: 'text-red-400',
    button: 'from-red-500 to-red-700 hover:from-red-400 hover:to-red-600',
    buttonShadow: 'shadow-red-500/30',
  },
  warning: {
    Icon: AlertTriangle,
    iconBg: 'bg-orange-500/15 border border-orange-400/40',
    iconColor: 'text-orange-400',
    button: 'from-orange-500 to-orange-700 hover:from-orange-400 hover:to-orange-600',
    buttonShadow: 'shadow-orange-500/30',
  },
  info: {
    Icon: LogOut,
    iconBg: 'bg-blue-500/15 border border-blue-400/40',
    iconColor: 'text-blue-400',
    button: 'from-blue-500 to-blue-700 hover:from-blue-400 hover:to-blue-600',
    buttonShadow: 'shadow-blue-500/30',
  },
};

export function ConfirmDialog({
  open,
  title,
  message,
  confirmLabel = 'PotwierdĹş',
  cancelLabel = 'Anuluj',
  variant = 'default',
  icon,
  loading = false,
  onConfirm,
  onCancel,
}: Props) {
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false);

  // Refy trzymajÄ… aktualne wartoĹ›ci bez zmiany rozmiaru tablicy zaleĹĽnoĹ›ci
  const loadingRef = useRef(loading);
  const onCancelRef = useRef(onCancel);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadingRef.current = loading;
  }, [loading]);

  useEffect(() => {
    onCancelRef.current = onCancel;
  }, [onCancel]);

  // Portal dostÄ™pny po montaĹĽu
  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true);
  }, []);

  // ESC zamyka + blokada scrolla tĹ‚a.
  // Uwaga: tablica zaleĹĽnoĹ›ci ma STAĹY rozmiar [open] - dziÄ™ki refom nie musimy
  // dorzucaÄ‡ `loading` i `onCancel`, co powodowaĹ‚o bĹ‚Ä…d w HMR.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;

    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape' && !loadingRef.current) {
        onCancelRef.current();
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    const prevOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';

    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
      document.body.style.overflow = prevOverflow;
    };
  }, [open]);

  if (!open || !mounted) return null;

  const style = VARIANT_STYLES[variant];
  const Icon = style.Icon;

  // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
  // AutoFocus: przy danger â†’ Cancel, reszta â†’ Confirm.
  //
  // Dlaczego: przy usuwaniu (Enter potwierdza) user moĹĽe
  // przypadkowo wcisnÄ…Ä‡ Enter po wpisaniu czegoĹ› i skasowaÄ‡
  // dane. Bezpieczniej ĹĽeby focus byĹ‚ na Cancel.
  // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
  const focusOnCancel = variant === 'danger';

  return createPortal(
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center p-4 animate-fadeIn"
      onClick={() => !loading && onCancel()}
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
    >
      {/* Backdrop */}
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-slate-950/80 backdrop-blur-md" />

      {/* Dialog */}
      <div
        className="relative w-full max-w-sm bg-slate-900 border border-white/15 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-2xl shadow-black/80"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        {/* Ikona (custom lub z wariantu) */}
        <div
          className={`w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center mb-4 ${style.iconBg}`}
        >
          {icon ?? (
            <Icon className={`w-6 h-6 ${style.iconColor}`} strokeWidth={2.5} />
          )}
        </div>

        {/* TytuĹ‚ */}
        <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white mb-2">{title}</h2>

        {/* TreĹ›Ä‡ */}
        <div className="text-sm text-white/70 leading-relaxed mb-5">
          {message}
        </div>

        {/* Przyciski */}
        <div className="flex gap-2.5">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onCancel}
            disabled={loading}
            autoFocus={focusOnCancel && !loading}
            className="flex-1 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 border border-white/10 hover:border-white/20 text-white font-semibold text-sm transition-all active:scale-95 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:active:scale-100 focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-white/40"
          >
            {cancelLabel}
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onConfirm}
            disabled={loading}
            autoFocus={!focusOnCancel && !loading}
            className={`flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br ${style.button} text-white font-semibold text-sm transition-all active:scale-95 shadow-lg ${style.buttonShadow} disabled:opacity-70 disabled:cursor-wait disabled:active:scale-100 focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-white/40`}
          >
            {loading && <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />}
            {confirmLabel}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>,
    document.body
  );
}

'use client';

import { usePathname } from 'next/navigation';

export function ContentWrapper({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const pathname = usePathname();

  const noSidebar =
    pathname === '/' ||
    pathname.startsWith('/auth') ||
    pathname === '/prywatnosc' ||
    pathname === '/regulamin';

  return (
    <div className={noSidebar ? '' : 'md:pl-60'}>
      {children}
    </div>
  );

=== PLIK: C:\Users\AdamPilny\Desktop\talent\Aplikacja-trenera-pi-karskiego-main\components\DatePicker.tsx ===
'use client';

import { useState, useRef, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import { Calendar as CalendarIcon, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, ChevronDown } from 'lucide-react';

const MIESIACE = [
  'StyczeĹ„', 'Luty', 'Marzec', 'KwiecieĹ„', 'Maj', 'Czerwiec',
  'Lipiec', 'SierpieĹ„', 'WrzesieĹ„', 'PaĹşdziernik', 'Listopad', 'GrudzieĹ„',
];
const DNI = ['Pn', 'Wt', 'Ĺšr', 'Cz', 'Pt', 'Sb', 'Nd'];

const pad2 = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
const toISO = (y: number, m: number, d: number) => `${y}-${pad2(m + 1)}-${pad2(d)}`;
const parseISO = (iso: string) => {
  const [y, m, d] = iso.split('-').map(Number);
  return new Date(y, m - 1, d);
};
const daysInMonth = (y: number, m: number) => new Date(y, m + 1, 0).getDate();
const firstWeekdayMon = (y: number, m: number) => {
  const d = new Date(y, m, 1).getDay();
  return d === 0 ? 6 : d - 1;
};
const formatDisplay = (iso: string) => {
  if (!iso) return '';
  const d = parseISO(iso);
  return `${pad2(d.getDate())}.${pad2(d.getMonth() + 1)}.${d.getFullYear()}`;
};

const PANEL_HEIGHT = 420;
const PANEL_MIN_WIDTH = 300;
const MARGIN = 8;

export function DatePicker({
  name,
  defaultValue,
  value: controlledValue,
  onChange,
  required,
  placeholder = 'Wybierz datÄ™',
}: {
  name?: string;
  defaultValue?: string;
  value?: string;
  onChange?: (iso: string) => void;
  required?: boolean;
  placeholder?: string;
}) {
  const isControlled = controlledValue !== undefined;
  const [internalValue, setInternalValue] = useState(defaultValue ?? '');
  const value = isControlled ? controlledValue : internalValue;

  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false);
  const [coords, setCoords] = useState({
    top: 0,
    left: 0,
    width: 0,
    placeAbove: false,
  });
  const buttonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const panelRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const initialDate = value ? parseISO(value) : new Date();
  const [viewYear, setViewYear] = useState(initialDate.getFullYear());
  const [viewMonth, setViewMonth] = useState(initialDate.getMonth());

  const dzisISO = useMemo(() => {
    const d = new Date();
    return toISO(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), d.getDate());
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true);
  }, []);

  const updateCoords = () => {
    if (!buttonRef.current) return;
    const rect = buttonRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
    const panelHeight = panelRef.current?.offsetHeight ?? PANEL_HEIGHT;
    const spaceBelow = window.innerHeight - rect.bottom;
    const placeAbove = spaceBelow < panelHeight + 20 && rect.top > panelHeight + 20;

    // SzerokoĹ›Ä‡ panelu: co najmniej PANEL_MIN_WIDTH, ĹĽeby siatka 7 dni
    // siÄ™ nie rozjeĹĽdĹĽaĹ‚a na wÄ…skich przyciskach (np. w gridzie 2-kolumnowym).
    const panelWidth = Math.max(rect.width, PANEL_MIN_WIDTH);

    // Clamp lewej krawÄ™dzi, ĹĽeby panel nie wychodziĹ‚ poza viewport.
    const maxLeft = window.innerWidth - panelWidth - MARGIN;
    const clampedLeft = Math.max(MARGIN, Math.min(rect.left, maxLeft));

    setCoords({
      top: placeAbove ? rect.top - panelHeight - 8 : rect.bottom + 8,
      left: clampedLeft,
      width: panelWidth,
      placeAbove,
    });
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    updateCoords();
    const onResize = () => updateCoords();
    const onScroll = () => updateCoords();
    window.addEventListener('resize', onResize);
    window.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, true);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('resize', onResize);
      window.removeEventListener('scroll', onScroll, true);
    };
  }, [open]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const handler = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      const t = e.target as Node;
      if (buttonRef.current?.contains(t) || panelRef.current?.contains(t)) return;
      setOpen(false);
    };
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handler);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handler);
  }, [open]);

  const setValue = (iso: string) => {
    if (!isControlled) setInternalValue(iso);
    onChange?.(iso);
  };

  const selectDay = (d: number) => {
    const iso = toISO(viewYear, viewMonth, d);
    setValue(iso);
    setOpen(false);
  };

  const changeMonth = (delta: number) => {
    let m = viewMonth + delta;
    let y = viewYear;
    if (m > 11) { m = 0; y++; }
    if (m < 0) { m = 11; y--; }
    setViewMonth(m);
    setViewYear(y);
  };

  const goToday = () => {
    const d = new Date();
    setViewYear(d.getFullYear());
    setViewMonth(d.getMonth());
    selectDay(d.getDate());
  };

  const wyczysc = () => {
    setValue('');
    setOpen(false);
  };

  const renderDni = () => {
    const cells: React.ReactNode[] = [];
    const firstDay = firstWeekdayMon(viewYear, viewMonth);
    const totalDays = daysInMonth(viewYear, viewMonth);
    const prevMonthDays = daysInMonth(viewYear, viewMonth - 1);

    for (let i = firstDay - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const d = prevMonthDays - i;
      cells.push(
        <div
          key={`prev-${d}`}
          className="aspect-square flex items-center justify-center text-xs text-white/20"
        >
          {d}
        </div>
      );
    }

    for (let d = 1; d <= totalDays; d++) {
      const iso = toISO(viewYear, viewMonth, d);
      const isSelected = iso === value;
      const isToday = iso === dzisISO;
      cells.push(
        <button
          key={iso}
          type="button"
          onClick={() => selectDay(d)}
          className={`aspect-square rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center text-sm font-semibold transition-all ${
            isSelected
              ? 'bg-green-500 text-white shadow-lg shadow-green-500/40'
              : isToday
              ? 'bg-green-500/15 text-green-400 border border-green-400/50'
              : 'text-white/80 hover:bg-white/10 hover:text-white'
          }`}
        >
          {d}
        </button>
      );
    }

    const remaining = (7 - (cells.length % 7)) % 7;
    for (let d = 1; d <= remaining; d++) {
      cells.push(
        <div
          key={`next-${d}`}
          className="aspect-square flex items-center justify-center text-xs text-white/20"
        >
          {d}
        </div>
      );
    }
    return cells;
  };

  return (
    <>
      {name && <input type="hidden" name={name} value={value} required={required} />}

      <button
        ref={buttonRef}
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}
        className="w-full flex items-center gap-2 text-left py-2 text-white"
      >
        <span className={value ? 'font-semibold' : 'text-white/40'}>
          {value ? formatDisplay(value) : placeholder}
        </span>
        <ChevronDown
          className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 text-white/40 ml-auto flex-shrink-0 transition-transform ${
            open ? 'rotate-180' : ''
          }`}
        />
      </button>

      {mounted &&
        open &&
        createPortal(
          <div
            ref={panelRef}
            style={{
              position: 'fixed',
              top: `${coords.top}px`,
              left: `${coords.left}px`,
              width: `${coords.width}px`,
              zIndex: 9999,
            }}
            className="bg-slate-900 border border-white/15 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl shadow-black/80 overflow-hidden"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 border-b border-white/10">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => changeMonth(-1)}
                className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/15 border border-white/10 flex items-center justify-center text-white/70 hover:text-white transition-colors"
              >
                <ChevronLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>

              <div className="text-center">
                <div className="text-sm font-bold text-white">
                  {MIESIACE[viewMonth]} {viewYear}
                </div>
                <div className="text-[10px] text-white/40 uppercase tracking-widest">
                  {viewYear === new Date().getFullYear() && viewMonth === new Date().getMonth() ? 'BieĹĽÄ…cy miesiÄ…c' : ''}
                </div>
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => changeMonth(1)}
                className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/15 border border-white/10 flex items-center justify-center text-white/70 hover:text-white transition-colors"
              >
                <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-7 gap-1 px-3 pt-3 pb-1">
              {DNI.map((d) => (
                <div
                  key={d}
                  className="text-center text-[10px] font-bold text-white/40 uppercase tracking-wider"
                >
                  {d}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-7 gap-1 px-3 pb-2">
              {renderDni()}
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 border-t border-white/10 bg-slate-950/40">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={wyczysc}
                className="text-xs font-semibold text-white/50 hover:text-white transition-colors"
              >
                WyczyĹ›Ä‡
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={goToday}
                className="text-xs font-bold text-green-400 hover:text-green-300 transition-colors"
              >
                DziĹ›
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>,
          document.body
        )}
    </>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { usunKonto } from '@/app/(app)/profil/actions';
import { ConfirmDialog } from '@/components/ConfirmDialog';
import { Trash2, Loader2, AlertTriangle } from 'lucide-react';

export function DeleteAccountButton() {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleDelete = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);

    const result = await usunKonto();

    if (result?.error) {
      setError(result.error);
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (

        onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
        disabled={loading}
        className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-red-500/10 hover:bg-red-500/20 border border-red-400/30 hover:border-red-400/60 text-red-300 hover:text-red-200 font-semibold py-3.5 rounded-2xl text-sm transition-all disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-wait"
      >
        {loading ? (
          <>
            <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />
            Usuwanie konta...
          </>
        ) : (
          <>
            <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
            UsuĹ„ konto
          </>
        )}
      </button>

      <ConfirmDialog
        open={open}
        title="UsunÄ…Ä‡ konto?"
        variant="danger"
        confirmLabel={loading ? 'Usuwanie...' : 'UsuĹ„ konto'}
        cancelLabel="Anuluj"
        loading={loading}
        icon={<AlertTriangle className="w-6 h-6 text-red-400" strokeWidth={2.5} />}
        message={
          <div className="space-y-3">

=== PLIK: C:\Users\AdamPilny\Desktop\talent\Aplikacja-trenera-pi-karskiego-main\components\DeleteConfirmButton.tsx ===
'use client';

import { useState, useTransition } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { ConfirmDialog } from '@/components/ConfirmDialog';

type Props = {
  id: number | string;
  onDelete: (id: number | string) => Promise<{ error?: string; success?: boolean } | void>;
  title: string;
  description: React.ReactNode;
  confirmLabel?: string;
  buttonLabel?: string;
  variant?: 'icon' | 'full';
  redirectTo?: string;
  successMessage?: string;
};

export function DeleteConfirmButton({
  id,
  onDelete,
  title,
  description,
  confirmLabel = 'UsuĹ„',
  buttonLabel = 'UsuĹ„',
  variant = 'full',
  redirectTo,
  successMessage = 'UsuniÄ™to',
}: Props) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isPending, startTransition] = useTransition();
  const router = useRouter();

  const handleDelete = () => {
    setOpen(false);
    startTransition(async () => {
      try {
        const result = await onDelete(id);
        if (result && 'error' in result && result.error) {
          toast.error('Nie udaĹ‚o siÄ™ usunÄ…Ä‡', { description: result.error });
          return;
        }
        toast.success(successMessage);
        if (redirectTo) {
          router.push(redirectTo);
        }
        router.refresh();
      } catch (err) {
        toast.error('Nie udaĹ‚o siÄ™ usunÄ…Ä‡', {
          description: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Nieznany bĹ‚Ä…d',
        });
      }
    });
  };

  const buttonClass =
    variant === 'icon'
      ? 'w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-red-500/5 hover:bg-red-500/20 border border-red-400/20 hover:border-red-400/50 flex items-center justify-center text-red-400/60 hover:text-red-300 transition-all disabled:opacity-50'
      : 'flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-gradient-to-br from-red-500 to-red-700 hover:from-red-400 hover:to-red-600 text-white font-bold px-6 py-3.5 rounded-2xl transition-all hover:shadow-xl hover:shadow-red-500/50 hover:-translate-y-0.5 border border-white/20 disabled:opacity-70 disabled:cursor-wait';

  return (
    <>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
        disabled={isPending}
        className={buttonClass}
        title={buttonLabel}
        aria-label={buttonLabel}
      >
        <Trash2 className={variant === 'icon' ? 'w-4 h-4' : 'w-4 h-4'} />
        {variant === 'full' && <span>{isPending ? 'Usuwanie...' : buttonLabel}</span>}
      </button>

      <ConfirmDialog
        open={open}
        title={title}
        variant="danger"
        confirmLabel={isPending ? 'Usuwanie...' : confirmLabel}
        cancelLabel="Anuluj"
        loading={isPending}
        onCancel={() => setOpen(false)}
        onConfirm={handleDelete}
        message={description}
      />
    </>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Info } from 'lucide-react';
import Link from 'next/link';

export function DemoBanner() {
  const [isDemo, setIsDemo] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    // SprawdĹş URL i/lub cookie
    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    const urlDemo = params.get('demo') === 'true';

    // SprawdĹş czy to konto demo (po cookie/email)
    const cookies = document.cookie.split(';').map((c) => c.trim());
    const hasDemoFlag = cookies.some((c) => c.startsWith('demo_mode='));

    setIsDemo(urlDemo || hasDemoFlag);
  }, []);

  if (!isDemo) return null;

  return (
    <div className="w-full bg-blue-500/20 border-b border-blue-400/40 backdrop-blur-md py-2 px-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold text-blue-200 sticky top-0 z-50 shadow-lg shadow-blue-500/10">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <Info className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0" />
        <span>Tryb demo â€” dane przykĹ‚adowe, zmiany nie sÄ… trwaĹ‚e.</span>
      </div>
      <Link
        href="/auth/sign-up"
        className="underline hover:text-white transition-colors"
      >
        ZaĹ‚ĂłĹĽ wĹ‚asne konto â†’
      </Link>
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useState, useRef, useEffect, useTransition } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { ChevronDown, Check, Plus, Settings } from 'lucide-react';
import { setActiveDruzyna } from '@/lib/druzyny-actions';

type Druzyna = {
  id: string;
  nazwa: string;
  rocznik: number | null;
  kolor: string;
  logo_url?: string | null;
};

const KOLORY: Record<string, string> = {
  green: 'bg-green-500',
  blue: 'bg-blue-500',
  purple: 'bg-purple-500',
  orange: 'bg-orange-500',
  red: 'bg-red-500',
  yellow: 'bg-yellow-500',
  pink: 'bg-pink-500',
  slate: 'bg-slate-500',
};

export function DruzynaSwitcher({
  druzyny,
  activeId,
  zawodnicyCount = {},
}: {
  druzyny: Druzyna[];
  activeId: string;
  zawodnicyCount?: Record<string, number>;
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false);
  const [coords, setCoords] = useState({ top: 0, right: 0 });
  const [isPending, startTransition] = useTransition();
  const router = useRouter();
  const buttonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const panelRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const active = druzyny.find((d) => d.id === activeId) ?? druzyny[0];
  const activeColor = KOLORY[active?.kolor ?? 'green'] ?? KOLORY.green;

  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true);
  }, []);

  const updateCoords = () => {
    if (!buttonRef.current) return;
    const rect = buttonRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
    setCoords({
      top: rect.bottom + 8,
      right: window.innerWidth - rect.right,
    });
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    updateCoords();

    const onResize = () => updateCoords();
    const onScroll = () => updateCoords();

    window.addEventListener('resize', onResize);
    window.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, true);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('resize', onResize);
      window.removeEventListener('scroll', onScroll, true);
    };
  }, [open]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const handler = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      const target = e.target as Node;
      if (
        buttonRef.current?.contains(target) ||
        panelRef.current?.contains(target)
      ) {
        return;
      }
      setOpen(false);
    };
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handler);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handler);
  }, [open]);

  const handleSelect = (id: string) => {
    if (id === activeId) {
      setOpen(false);
      return;
    }
    startTransition(async () => {
      await setActiveDruzyna(id);
      setOpen(false);
      router.refresh();
    });
  };

  if (!active) return null;

  return (
    <>
      <button
        ref={buttonRef}
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}
        disabled={isPending}
        className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 border transition-all flex-shrink-0 max-w-[160px] md:max-w-[220px] ${
          open
            ? 'bg-white/10 border-white/25 ring-2 ring-green-400/30'
            : 'border-white/10 hover:border-white/20'
        } ${isPending ? 'opacity-70 cursor-wait' : ''}`}
        aria-label="ZmieĹ„ druĹĽynÄ™"
      >
        {/* Zamiast logo - wracamy do kolorowej kropki */}
        <span className={`w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full ${activeColor} flex-shrink-0`} />
        
        <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-white/90 whitespace-nowrap truncate">
          {active.nazwa}
        </span>
        <ChevronDown
          className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 text-white/50 flex-shrink-0 transition-transform ${
            open ? 'rotate-180' : ''
          }`}
        />
      </button>

      {mounted &&
        open &&
        createPortal(
          <div
            ref={panelRef}
            style={{
              position: 'fixed',
              top: `${coords.top}px`,
              right: `${coords.right}px`,
              zIndex: 9999,
            }}
            className="w-64 bg-slate-900 border border-white/15 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl shadow-black/80 overflow-hidden"
          >
            <div className="px-3 py-2 border-b border-white/10 bg-slate-900">
              <div className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest font-bold text-white/40">
                Twoje druĹĽyny
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="max-h-64 overflow-y-auto bg-slate-900">
              {druzyny.map((d) => {
                const color = KOLORY[d.kolor] ?? KOLORY.green;
                const isActive = d.id === activeId;
                const count = zawodnicyCount[d.id] ?? 0;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={d.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleSelect(d.id)}
                    disabled={isPending}
                    className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 hover:bg-white/5 transition-colors text-left disabled:opacity-50"
                  >
                    {/* W liĹ›cie rozwijanej teĹĽ wracamy do kropki */}
                    <span className={`w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full ${color} flex-shrink-0`} />
                    
                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                      <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-white truncate">
                        {d.nazwa}
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-[10px] text-white/40">
                        {count === 0 ? 'brak zawodnikĂłw' : `${count} zawodnikĂłw`}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    {isActive && (
                      <Check
                        className="w-4 h-4 text-green-400 flex-shrink-0"
                        strokeWidth={3}
                      />
                    )}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>

            <div className="border-t border-white/10 bg-slate-900">
              <Link
                href="/druzyny"
                onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
                className="flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 hover:bg-white/5 transition-colors"
              >
                <div className="w-5 h-5 rounded-lg bg-white/10 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0">
                  <Settings className="w-3 h-3 text-white/60" />
                </div>
                <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-white/70">
                  ZarzÄ…dzaj druĹĽynami
                </span>
              </Link>
              <Link
                href="/druzyny/nowa"
                onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
                className="flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 hover:bg-white/5 transition-colors border-t border-white/5"
              >
                <div className="w-5 h-5 rounded-lg bg-gradient-to-br from-green-500 to-green-700 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0">
                  <Plus className="w-3 h-3 text-white" strokeWidth={3} />
                </div>
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-green-400">Nowa druĹĽyna</span>
              </Link>
            </div>
          </div>,
          document.body
        )}
    </>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { Mail, Send, AlertCircle, CheckCircle2, ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react';
import { AuthField } from '@/components/AuthField';
import { SubmitButton } from '@/components/SubmitButton';
import { forgotPasswordSchema, zodToFieldErrors } from '@/lib/schemas';
import { getErrorMessage } from '@/lib/errors';

export function ForgotPasswordForm() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [fieldErrors, setFieldErrors] = useState<{ email?: string }>({});
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);

  const supabase = useMemo(() => createClient(), []);

  const handleForgotPassword = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);

    const result = forgotPasswordSchema.safeParse({ email });
    if (!result.success) {
      setFieldErrors(zodToFieldErrors(result.error));
      return;
    }
    setFieldErrors({});

    setIsLoading(true);

    try {
      const { error } = await supabase.auth.resetPasswordForEmail(email, {
        redirectTo: `${window.location.origin}/auth/confirm`,
      });

      if (error) throw error;

      setSuccess(true);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(getErrorMessage(err));
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  if (success) {
    return (
      <div className="bg-slate-900/80 backdrop-blur-md border border-green-400/30 rounded-3xl p-6 shadow-2xl shadow-black/60 text-center">
        <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-green-400 via-green-500 to-green-700 shadow-2xl shadow-green-500/30 mb-4">
          <CheckCircle2 className="w-7 h-7 text-white" strokeWidth={2.5} />
        </div>
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white drop-shadow-lg mb-2">
          SprawdĹş maila

        </p>
        <p className="text-xs text-white/40 mb-6">
          Nie widzisz maila? SprawdĹş folder spam.

        <Link
          href="/auth/login"
          className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 w-full bg-gradient-to-br from-green-500 to-green-600 hover:from-green-400 hover:to-green-500 text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-2xl transition-all hover:shadow-xl hover:shadow-green-500/50 hover:-translate-y-0.5"

import { LucideIcon } from 'lucide-react';

export function FormField({
  icon: Icon,
  label,
  required,
  children,
}: {
  icon: LucideIcon;
  label: string;
  required?: boolean;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <label className="block group">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1.5">
        <Icon className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-white/50 group-focus-within:text-green-400 transition-colors" />
        <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-white/70 uppercase tracking-wider">
          {label}
          {required && <span className="text-green-400 ml-1">*</span>}
        </span>
      </div>
      <div className="bg-white/5 border border-white/15 focus-within:border-green-400/60 focus-within:bg-white/10 rounded-xl px-3 transition-all">
        {children}
      </div>
    </label>
  );

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';

export function GoogleSignInButton({
  nextPath = '/dashboard',
}: {
  nextPath?: string;
}) {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  async function handleSignIn() {
    setLoading(true);
    const supabase = createClient();

    const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithOAuth({
      provider: 'google',
      options: {
        redirectTo: `${window.location.origin}/auth/callback?next=${encodeURIComponent(nextPath)}`,
        // access_type: 'offline' + prompt: 'consent' â†’ Google wyda refresh token
        // Bez tego dostajesz tylko access token (wygasa po 60 min)
        queryParams: {
          access_type: 'offline',
          prompt: 'consent',
        },
      },
    });

    if (error) {
      console.error('Google OAuth error:', error);
      setLoading(false);
    }
    // Przy sukcesie przeglÄ…darka przekierowuje na Google - nie trzeba resetowaÄ‡ loading
  }

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={handleSignIn}
      disabled={loading}
      className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-3 bg-white hover:bg-gray-100 text-gray-800 font-semibold py-3.5 rounded-2xl transition-all hover:shadow-lg disabled:opacity-70 disabled:cursor-wait"
    >
      {loading ? (
        <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />
      ) : (
        <svg className="w-5 h-5" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
          <path fill="#4285F4" d="M22.56 12.25c0-.78-.07-1.53-.2-2.25H12v4.26h5.92c-.26 1.37-1.04 2.53-2.21 3.31v2.77h3.57c2.08-1.92 3.28-4.74 3.28-8.09z"/>
          <path fill="#34A853" d="M12 23c2.97 0 5.46-.98 7.28-2.66l-3.57-2.77c-.98.66-2.23 1.06-3.71 1.06-2.86 0-5.29-1.93-6.16-4.53H2.18v2.84C3.99 20.53 7.7 23 12 23z"/>
          <path fill="#FBBC05" d="M5.84 14.09c-.22-.66-.35-1.36-.35-2.09s.13-1.43.35-2.09V7.07H2.18C1.43 8.55 1 10.22 1 12s.43 3.45 1.18 4.93l2.85-2.22.81-.62z"/>
          <path fill="#EA4335" d="M12 5.38c1.62 0 3.06.56 4.21 1.64l3.15-3.15C17.45 2.09 14.97 1 12 1 7.7 1 3.99 3.47 2.18 7.07l3.66 2.84c.87-2.6 3.3-4.53 6.16-4.53z"/>
        </svg>
      )}
      {loading ? 'Przekierowanie...' : 'Kontynuuj z Google'}
    </button>
  );

'use client';

import { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { Mail, Lock, LogIn, AlertCircle, ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react';
import { AuthField } from '@/components/AuthField';
import { SubmitButton } from '@/components/SubmitButton';
import { GoogleSignInButton } from '@/components/GoogleSignInButton';
import { loginSchema, zodToFieldErrors } from '@/lib/schemas';
import { getErrorMessage } from '@/lib/errors';

export function LoginForm() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [fieldErrors, setFieldErrors] = useState<{
    email?: string;
    password?: string;
  }>({});
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const router = useRouter();

  const supabase = useMemo(() => createClient(), []);

  const handleLogin = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);

    const result = loginSchema.safeParse({ email, password });
    if (!result.success) {
      setFieldErrors(zodToFieldErrors(result.error));
      return;
    }
    setFieldErrors({});

    setIsLoading(true);

    const safetyTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      setIsLoading(false);
      setError('Przekierowanie trwa zbyt dĹ‚ugo. SprĂłbuj odĹ›wieĹĽyÄ‡ stronÄ™.');
    }, 10000);

    try {
      const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({
        email,
        password,
      });

      if (error) throw error;

      clearTimeout(safetyTimer);
      router.push('/dashboard');
      router.refresh();
    } catch (err) {
      clearTimeout(safetyTimer);
      setError(getErrorMessage(err));
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="w-full">
      <div className="bg-slate-900/80 backdrop-blur-md border border-white/10 rounded-3xl p-6 shadow-2xl shadow-black/60">
        <div className="text-center mb-6">
          <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-green-400 via-green-500 to-green-700 shadow-2xl shadow-green-500/30 mb-4">
            <LogIn className="w-7 h-7 text-white" strokeWidth={2.5} />
          </div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white drop-shadow-lg">
            Zaloguj siÄ™
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-white/60 mt-1">
            Wpisz email i hasĹ‚o, ĹĽeby wejĹ›Ä‡ do aplikacji
          </p>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleLogin} noValidate className="space-y-4">
          {/* Google OAuth */}
          <GoogleSignInButton nextPath="/dashboard" />

          {/* Separator */}
          <div className="relative my-2">
            <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center">
              <div className="w-full border-t border-white/10" />
            </div>
            <div className="relative flex justify-center text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest">
              <span className="bg-slate-900/80 px-3 text-white/40 font-bold">
                lub
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <AuthField
            icon={Mail}
            label="Email"
            name="email"
            id="login-email"
            type="email"
            autoComplete="email"
            value={email}
            onChange={(v) => {
              setEmail(v);
              if (fieldErrors.email) setFieldErrors((fe) => ({ ...fe, email: undefined }));
            }}
            placeholder="trener@example.com"
            error={fieldErrors.email}
          />

          <AuthField
            icon={Lock}
            label="HasĹ‚o"
            name="password"
            id="login-password"
            type="password"
            autoComplete="current-password"
            value={password}
            onChange={(v) => {
              setPassword(v);
              if (fieldErrors.password) setFieldErrors((fe) => ({ ...fe, password: undefined }));
            }}
            placeholder="Wpisz hasĹ‚o"
            error={fieldErrors.password}
            labelRight={
              <Link
                href="/auth/forgot-password"
                className="text-[10px] text-green-400 hover:text-green-300 font-semibold transition-colors"
              >
                ZapomniaĹ‚eĹ›?
              </Link>
            }
          />

          {error && (
            <div
              role="alert"
              className="bg-red-500/15 border border-red-400/40 rounded-xl p-3 flex items-start gap-2 text-xs text-red-200 animate-fadeIn"
            >
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
              <span>{error}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <SubmitButton
            icon={LogIn}
            label="Zaloguj siÄ™"
            loadingLabel="Logowanie..."
            loading={isLoading}
          />
        </form>

        <div className="mt-6 pt-5 border-t border-white/10 text-center">
          <p className="text-xs text-white/50">
            Nie masz konta?{' '}

'use client';

import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { LogOut } from 'lucide-react';

export function LogoutButton() {
  const handleLogout = async () => {
    const ok = window.confirm('WylogowaÄ‡ siÄ™?');
    if (!ok) return;

    const supabase = createClient();

    window.location.href = '/auth/login';
  };

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={handleLogout}
      className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-red-500/10 hover:bg-red-500/20 border border-red-400/30 hover:border-red-400/60 text-red-300 hover:text-red-200 font-semibold py-3.5 rounded-2xl text-sm transition-all"

'use client';

import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Calendar as CalendarIcon, ChevronDown, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';

type Miesiac = {
  iso: string;
  label: string;
  treningi: number;
  procent: number;
};

export function MiesiacDropdown({
  miesiace,
  aktywny,
  sort,
}: {
  miesiace: Miesiac[];
  aktywny: string | null;
  sort: string;
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const handler = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setOpen(false);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handler);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handler);
  }, [open]);

  const buildHref = (mm: string) => {
    const p = new URLSearchParams();
    p.set('okres', 'month');
    p.set('m', mm);
    if (sort && sort !== 'obecnosci') p.set('sort', sort);
    return `/statystyki?${p.toString()}`;
  };

  const aktywnyLabel = aktywny
    ? miesiace.find((mm) => mm.iso === aktywny)?.label ?? 'Konkretny miesiÄ…c'
    : 'Konkretny miesiÄ…c';

  const isActive = !!aktywny;

  return (
    <div ref={ref} className="relative">
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}
        className={`px-3 md:px-4 py-2 rounded-xl border text-xs md:text-sm font-semibold transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
          isActive
            ? 'bg-green-500/15 border-green-400/50 text-green-400'
            : 'bg-white/5 border-white/10 text-white/70 hover:bg-white/10 hover:border-white/25'
        }`}
      >
        <CalendarIcon className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
        {aktywnyLabel}
        <ChevronDown
          className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 transition-transform ${open ? 'rotate-180' : ''}`}
        />
      </button>

      {open && (
        <div className="absolute top-full left-0 mt-2 w-72 bg-slate-900 border border-white/15 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl shadow-black/80 overflow-hidden z-50">
          <div className="px-3 py-2 border-b border-white/10">
            <div className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest font-bold text-white/40">
              Wybierz miesiÄ…c
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="max-h-72 overflow-y-auto">
            {miesiace.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="px-3 py-4 text-xs text-white/40 text-center">
                Brak treningĂłw w historii
              </div>
            ) : (
              miesiace.map((mm) => {
                const isActiveItem = aktywny === mm.iso;
                return (
                  <Link
                    key={mm.iso}
                    href={buildHref(mm.iso)}
                    onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
                    className={`flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 transition-colors text-left ${
                      isActiveItem
                        ? 'bg-green-500/10 border-l-2 border-green-500'
                        : 'hover:bg-white/5'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                      <div
                        className={`text-sm font-semibold truncate ${
                          isActiveItem ? 'text-green-400' : 'text-white/90'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {mm.label}
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-[10px] text-white/40">
                        {mm.treningi}{' '}
                        {mm.treningi === 1 ? 'trening' : 'treningĂłw'} Â·{' '}
                        {mm.procent}% frekwencji
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    {isActiveItem && (
                      <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-green-400 flex-shrink-0" />
                    )}
                  </Link>
                );
              })
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Calendar as CalendarIcon, ChevronDown, Check } from 'lucide-react';

type Preset = { key: string; label: string };
type Miesiac = { iso: string; label: string; treningi: number; procent: number };

export function OkresDropdown({
  presety,
  miesiace,
  aktywnyPreset,
  aktywnyMiesiac,
}: {
  presety: Preset[];
  miesiace: Miesiac[];
  aktywnyPreset: string | null;
  aktywnyMiesiac: string | null;
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const handler = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setOpen(false);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handler);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handler);
  }, [open]);

  const buildHref = (o: string, mm?: string) => {
    const p = new URLSearchParams();
    p.set('okres', o);
    if (mm) p.set('m', mm);
    return `/statystyki?${p.toString()}`;
  };

  const displayLabel = aktywnyMiesiac
    ? miesiace.find((mm) => mm.iso === aktywnyMiesiac)?.label ?? 'Wybierz okres'
    : presety.find((p) => p.key === aktywnyPreset)?.label ?? 'Wybierz okres';

  return (
    <div ref={ref} className="relative w-full">
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}
        className={`w-full flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl border transition-all ${
          open
            ? 'bg-white/10 border-white/25 ring-2 ring-green-400/30'
            : 'bg-white/5 border-white/10 hover:border-white/20'
        }`}
      >
        <CalendarIcon className="w-4 h-4 text-green-400 flex-shrink-0" />
        <span className="text-xs font-bold text-white flex-1 text-left truncate">
          {displayLabel}
        </span>
        <ChevronDown
          className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 text-white/50 flex-shrink-0 transition-transform ${
            open ? 'rotate-180' : ''
          }`}
        />
      </button>

      {open && (
        <div className="absolute top-full left-0 right-0 mt-2 bg-slate-900 border border-white/15 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl shadow-black/80 overflow-hidden z-50">
          {/* Szybkie presety */}
          <div className="px-3 py-2 border-b border-white/10 bg-slate-950/40">
            <div className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest font-bold text-white/40">
              Szybkie
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            {presety.map((p) => {
              const isActive = !aktywnyMiesiac && aktywnyPreset === p.key;
              return (
                <Link
                  key={p.key}
                  href={buildHref(p.key)}
                  onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
                  className={`flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 transition-colors text-left ${
                    isActive
                      ? 'bg-green-500/10 border-l-2 border-green-500'
                      : 'hover:bg-white/5'
                  }`}
                >
                  <span
                    className={`flex-1 text-sm font-semibold ${
                      isActive ? 'text-green-400' : 'text-white/90'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {p.label}
                  </span>
                  {isActive && (
                    <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-green-400 flex-shrink-0" strokeWidth={3} />
                  )}
                </Link>
              );
            })}
          </div>

          {/* Konkretne miesiÄ…ce */}
          {miesiace.length > 0 && (
            <>
              <div className="px-3 py-2 border-y border-white/10 bg-slate-950/40">
                <div className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest font-bold text-white/40">
                  Konkretny miesiÄ…c
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="max-h-64 overflow-y-auto">
                {miesiace.map((mm) => {
                  const isActive = aktywnyMiesiac === mm.iso;
                  return (
                    <Link
                      key={mm.iso}
                      href={buildHref('month', mm.iso)}
                      onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
                      className={`flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 transition-colors text-left ${
                        isActive
                          ? 'bg-green-500/10 border-l-2 border-green-500'

import type { LucideIcon } from 'lucide-react';

export function PageHeaderLogo({
  icon: Icon,
  logoUrl,
  color = 'green',
}: {
  icon: LucideIcon;
  logoUrl: string | null;
  color?: 'green' | 'blue' | 'purple' | 'orange' | 'red';
}) {
  const gradientMap = {
    green: 'from-green-400 via-green-500 to-green-700',
    blue: 'from-blue-400 via-blue-500 to-blue-700',
    purple: 'from-purple-400 via-purple-500 to-purple-700',
    orange: 'from-orange-400 via-orange-500 to-orange-700',
    red: 'from-red-400 via-red-500 to-red-700',
  };

  const gradient = gradientMap[color];

  return (
    <div className="relative group cursor-pointer flex-shrink-0">

        }`}

'use client';

import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import { Download, X, Share, Plus } from 'lucide-react';

interface BeforeInstallPromptEvent extends Event {
  prompt: () => Promise<void>;
  userChoice: Promise<{ outcome: 'accepted' | 'dismissed' }>;
}

const DISMISS_KEY = 'pwa_install_dismissed_at';
const VISITS_KEY = 'pwa_visit_count';
const VISITS_BEFORE_PROMPT = 2;
const DISMISS_COOLDOWN_DAYS = 30;

function getVisitCount(): number {
  try {
    return parseInt(localStorage.getItem(VISITS_KEY) ?? '0', 10) || 0;
  } catch {
    return 0;
  }
}

function isDismissedRecently(): boolean {
  try {
    const at = localStorage.getItem(DISMISS_KEY);
    if (!at) return false;
    const days = (Date.now() - parseInt(at, 10)) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24);
    return days < DISMISS_COOLDOWN_DAYS;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

function isStandaloneMode(): boolean {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return false;
  if (window.matchMedia('(display-mode: standalone)').matches) return true;
  return (window.navigator as unknown as { standalone?: boolean }).standalone === true;
}

function isIOS(): boolean {
  if (typeof navigator === 'undefined') return false;
  return (
    /iPad|iPhone|iPod/.test(navigator.userAgent) &&
    !(window as unknown as { MSStream?: unknown }).MSStream
  );
}

export function PWAInstallPrompt() {
  const [deferredPrompt, setDeferredPrompt] =
    useState<BeforeInstallPromptEvent | null>(null);
  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(false);
  const [showIOSInstructions, setShowIOSInstructions] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    // WYĹÄ„CZONE W TRYBIE DEMO
    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    if (params.get('demo') === 'true') return;

    if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production') return;
    if (isStandaloneMode()) return;
    if (isDismissedRecently()) return;

    const visits = getVisitCount() + 1;
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(VISITS_KEY, String(visits));
    } catch {}

    if (visits < VISITS_BEFORE_PROMPT) return;

    if (isIOS()) {
      setVisible(true);
      return;
    }

    const handler = (e: Event) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      setDeferredPrompt(e as BeforeInstallPromptEvent);
      setVisible(true);
    };

    window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', handler);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', handler);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handler = () => {
      setVisible(false);
      try {
        localStorage.removeItem(DISMISS_KEY);
      } catch {}
    };
    window.addEventListener('appinstalled', handler);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('appinstalled', handler);
  }, []);

  const handleDismiss = useCallback(() => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(DISMISS_KEY, String(Date.now()));
    } catch {}
    setVisible(false);
  }, []);

  const handleInstall = useCallback(async () => {
    if (isIOS()) {
      setShowIOSInstructions(true);
      return;
    }
    if (!deferredPrompt) return;
    await deferredPrompt.prompt();
    const { outcome } = await deferredPrompt.userChoice;
    if (outcome === 'accepted') {
      setVisible(false);
    } else {
      handleDismiss();
    }
    setDeferredPrompt(null);
  }, [deferredPrompt, handleDismiss]);

  if (!visible) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed z-40 bottom-24 left-4 right-20 md:bottom-6 md:left-[16.5rem] md:right-auto md:w-80 animate-fadeIn">
      <div className="relative bg-slate-900/95 backdrop-blur-xl border border-green-400/40 rounded-2xl p-4 shadow-2xl shadow-black/60">
        <button
          onClick={handleDismiss}
          aria-label="Zamknij"
          className="absolute top-2 right-2 w-6 h-6 rounded-lg hover:bg-white/10 flex items-center justify-center text-white/40 hover:text-white transition-colors"
        >
          <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
        </button>

        {!showIOSInstructions ? (
          <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-green-400 to-green-700 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 shadow-lg shadow-green-500/30">
              <Download className="w-5 h-5 text-white" strokeWidth={2.5} />
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 pr-5">
              <div className="font-bold text-white text-sm">Zainstaluj Talent</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-white/60 mt-1 leading-relaxed">
                Szybki dostÄ™p z ekranu gĹ‚Ăłwnego. DziaĹ‚a jak natywna aplikacja.
              </div>
              <div className="flex gap-2 mt-3">
                <button
                  onClick={handleInstall}
                  className="flex-1 bg-gradient-to-br from-green-500 to-green-600 hover:from-green-400 hover:to-green-500 text-white font-bold text-xs py-2 rounded-lg transition-all hover:shadow-lg hover:shadow-green-500/40"
                >
                  Zainstaluj
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={handleDismiss}
                  className="px-3 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 border border-white/10 text-white/70 hover:text-white text-xs font-semibold py-2 rounded-lg transition-all"
                >
                  Nie teraz
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div>
            <div className="font-bold text-white text-sm mb-3 pr-5">
              Zainstaluj na iPhone
            </div>
            <ol className="space-y-2.5 text-xs text-white/70">
              <li className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
                <span className="w-5 h-5 rounded-md bg-white/10 flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-bold text-white flex-shrink-0">1</span>
                <span>Kliknij <Share className="w-3.5 h-3.5 inline text-green-400 mx-0.5" /> <strong className="text-white">UdostÄ™pnij</strong> na dole Safari</span>
              </li>
              <li className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
                <span className="w-5 h-5 rounded-md bg-white/10 flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-bold text-white flex-shrink-0">2</span>
                <span>Wybierz <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5 inline text-green-400 mx-0.5" /> <strong className="text-white">Dodaj do ekranu poczÄ…tkowego</strong></span>
              </li>
              <li className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
                <span className="w-5 h-5 rounded-md bg-white/10 flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-bold text-white flex-shrink-0">3</span>
                <span>Kliknij <strong className="text-white">Dodaj</strong> w prawym gĂłrnym rogu</span>
              </li>
            </ol>
            <button
              onClick={handleDismiss}
              className="w-full mt-4 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 border border-white/10 text-white/70 hover:text-white text-xs font-semibold py-2 rounded-lg transition-all"
            >
              Rozumiem
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
